# Coglossa M4b — The Web App: Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task by task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The reviewer's web app (spec §8), served by the M4a API and tested against it. It has six screens:
- **Projects:** create projects with their languages and profile.
- **Documents and runs:** upload a document, follow its run live, and cancel or retry it.
- **Guide editor:** edit the guide, approve it at the checkpoint, and re-run the sweep after a locked term changes.
- **Review view:** source | translation | meaning | style | flags. It has filters, keyboard navigation between flags, a panel per passage, and every review action.
- **Export:** the four downloads.
- **System:** `doctor`, the config and the language tiers.

Its TypeScript types are generated from `schema/openapi.json`, and CI fails on drift. Vitest tests cover at least 95% of lines and branches, and Playwright runs the journey upload → approve guide → review and resolve a flag → export against a real local stack.

Task 0 renames the "bible" to the **translation guide** throughout (the user's decision of 2026-10-11, for the first customer). Every other task uses the new names.

This plan also finishes the M4a carry-over items. Task 1 settles what "the project guide" means, so that approving a guide at the checkpoint works with several runs in a project. Task 2 has the API serve the app.

**Architecture:**
- **The app.** `web/` is a TypeScript React single-page app built with Vite (pnpm).
  - **Routing:** a react-router 7 data router, so unsaved guide edits can be guarded on navigation.
  - **Server state:** TanStack Query is the only cache. Mutations replace the data they return and invalidate exactly what they change.
  - **API calls:** every call goes through one `openapi-fetch` client typed by `src/api/schema.d.ts`. The two exceptions are URLs the browser loads itself (the progress `EventSource` and the download links), which are built by helpers in `src/api/client.ts`.
  - **Styling:** plain CSS, no UI library.
  - **Fonts:** Noto fonts from `@fontsource`, bundled.
- **The server.** The API serves the built app from `api.web_dir` (Task 2). It answers `index.html` for every path outside `/api`, so the client's routes survive a reload.
- **Tests:**
  - **Component tests** render the whole app at a URL (`renderApp`) and talk to `msw`, which stands in for the API with typed fixtures.
  - **Journeys:** Playwright drives a real stack (`api/devstack.py`): a throwaway Postgres database, the fake model and scorer servers, a worker, and the API serving the built app.

**Tech Stack:**
- **Runtime:** React 19.2, react-router 7, TanStack Query 5, openapi-fetch 0.14.
- **Build and types:** TypeScript 5.9 (strict), Vite 7.
- **Tests:** Vitest 3.2 with v8 coverage, Testing Library, msw 2, Playwright 1.56.
- **Lint and format:** ESLint 9, Prettier 3.
- **Tooling:** pnpm 10, Node ≥ 22.
- **Server side** (Tasks 1–2, 12): unchanged from M4a (Python, FastAPI, uv).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-coglossa-v1-design.md`. The relevant sections:
- §8 (every screen)
- §7.1 (SSE progress with a polling fallback)
- §7.4 (doctor on the System page)
- §7.5 (exports)
- §6.5 (feedback capture, through the M4a API)
- §2.1 (the API serves the SPA; TS types from the schema)
- §1.2 criterion 5 and §9.1 (Vitest + Testing Library + Playwright; 95% line and branch coverage for `web/`; the contract check in CI)
- §11 (milestone M4).

M4a is on `main`: the REST API, its OpenAPI document in `schema/openapi.json`, and `docs/m4a-followups.md`. The M4b carry-over items that this plan resolves are listed in Task 1, Task 2 and Task 12.

## Global Constraints

- **Every M1–M4a constraint still holds:**
  - 95% branch coverage per Python package
  - `pytest -W error` (now `filterwarnings = ["error"]` in the engine and the api)
  - `make lint` clean
  - `make schema` shows no drift
  - tests touch no network except `127.0.0.1`
  - the library boundary
  - scores are never invented
  - every user-facing message is one line.
- **`web/` is pinned exactly.** No `^` or `~`. `pnpm-lock.yaml` is committed, and CI installs with `--frozen-lockfile`. The versions are those in Task 3's `package.json`; each was published at least two weeks before this plan.
  - `packageManager` is `pnpm@10.32.1`, and Node is `>=22`. CI uses Node 22.
- **Coverage for `web/`.** Vitest v8 coverage, at least 95% on lines, branches, functions and statements, measured over `src/**/*.{ts,tsx}`. Coverage excludes:
  - the generated `src/api/schema.d.ts`
  - `src/main.tsx`, the page bootstrap, which the journeys cover
  - the test harness in `src/test/**`.

  Each exclusion carries a one-line justification comment in `vite.config.ts`.
- **The contract.** `src/api/schema.d.ts` is generated from `schema/openapi.json` by `pnpm gen:api`. `make schema` regenerates it, and CI fails when it differs. Test fixtures are typed with the generated types, so an API change that breaks a fixture fails `tsc`. The app never builds an API URL by hand except through `eventsUrl` and `exportUrl`.
- **Offline (spec §8).** No CDN and no external URL is ever requested. Fonts are bundled from `@fontsource`. The Playwright journey asserts that every request goes to the app's own origin.
- **Text direction (spec §8).** Every element that shows document or user text carries `dir="auto"`:
  - source, translation, candidates and back translation
  - guide entries, summary and revisions
  - project names and file names.
- **Language.** The UI language is English. The API's `detail` is shown as is; it is already one line and safe to show.
- **Labels and accessible names.** Every control has a label or an accessible name. Tests find elements by role and label (Testing Library), which keeps this true.
- **Same origin.** The app calls the server it was loaded from (`location.origin`). In development, Vite forwards `/api` to the local stack.
- **No server state outside TanStack Query.** Local React state holds only UI state: forms, the open panel and the selection.

## Review Focus

These are inputs the spec implies but no screen's main tests exercise. Each gets a test in the task named.

1. **Leaving the guide editor with unsaved edits** — by navigating inside the app, reloading or closing the tab. The app asks before discarding the edits, and the browser warns on unload. A save that succeeded releases the guard, so approving navigates without a prompt (Task 7: the guard dialog with "Stay" and "Leave", `beforeunload`, approve-then-navigate).
2. **Right-to-left and mixed scripts** (an Arabic project name, an Arabic source passage, a Hebrew or Arabic target). Each piece of text shows in its own direction (`dir="auto"`) and in a bundled font (Task 4: an Arabic project name; Task 5: file names; Task 9: an Arabic source cell; Task 7: guide fields; Task 10: candidates, back translation and history).
3. **A review action refused while the sweep runs, or by a concurrent change (409).** The message is shown, and what the reviewer typed stays (Task 10: a refused edit keeps the textarea's text). Saving a stale guide offers a reload (Task 8). Approving after another save offers the current project guide (Task 7).
4. **The connection drops during a long run, or the run ends while nobody watches.** The browser's EventSource reconnects by itself. A `progress` event with a new state refreshes the run. A terminal state closes the stream, and a deleted run shows the 404. Without EventSource the run is polled until it ends (Task 6: live updates, close on terminal, `deleted`, `pollInterval`).
5. **A long document.** Passages load 50 at a time. The filters and the page live in the URL, so reload, back and shared links keep the place. Keyboard navigation between flags crosses page boundaries (Task 9: paging, URL round trip, `n` across pages; Task 12: a reload keeps the filter).

- **M5:**
  - the Dockerfile that builds `web/dist` into the api image and sets `api.web_dir`
  - the air-gap install test
  - the operator guide's web section
- **Later:**

  - keyboard shortcuts for the review actions themselves
  - an unsaved-changes guard on the passage editor
  - paging the projects and documents lists
  - side-by-side run comparison (a `bench` concern in v1, spec §8.2).

**Notes for every web task:**
- **Before committing:** run `pnpm fmt` (Prettier and ESLint's fixes), then `pnpm lint`. The code blocks here are close to, but not always exactly, Prettier's output.
- **Where the generated types win:** when `tsc` says a generated type differs from what a code block assumes (a field optional rather than nullable, a request field with a default generated as required), the generated type is the truth. Adjust the code and the test fixture to it, and say so in your report.

### Task 0: Rename "bible" to "guide" everywhere

**Why.** The first customer's reviewers may translate scripture, so "bible" (translation-industry jargon) reads wrong to them and collides with real document content. On 2026-10-11 the user chose the name **translation guide**, shown as **Guide** in tabs and buttons ("Approve guide", "Add to guide", "Project guide"). Nothing is deployed yet, so the rename covers the code, the API, the database, the CLI, the config, the prompts and the docs. No compatibility aliases are kept.

Every later task in this plan already uses the new names.

**Files:**
- `git mv` these:

  | From | To |
  |---|---|
  | `api/coglossa_api/routes/bibles.py` | `api/coglossa_api/routes/guides.py` |
  | `api/tests/test_bibles.py` | `api/tests/test_guides.py` |
  | `engine/coglossa/prompts/templates/bible/` | `engine/coglossa/prompts/templates/guide/` |
  | `engine/coglossa/prompts/templates/bible_merge/` | `engine/coglossa/prompts/templates/guide_merge/` |
  | `engine/coglossa/schema/bible.py` | `engine/coglossa/schema/guide.py` |
  | `engine/coglossa/stages/bible.py` | `engine/coglossa/stages/guide.py` |
  | `engine/tests/stages/test_bible.py` | `engine/tests/stages/test_guide.py` |
  | `engine/tests/test_bibles.py` | `engine/tests/test_guides.py` |
  | `schema/bible.schema.json` | `schema/guide.schema.json` |

- Modify: every tracked text file that contains the word. There are two kinds of exclusion:
  - the finished plans, which are records of what was done at the time: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-09-m1-engine-core.md`, `2026-10-09-m2-scoring.md`, `2026-10-10-m3-persistence.md` and `2026-10-11-m4a-api.md`
  - this plan, which already uses the new names everywhere except in this task's instructions.
- This includes:
  - `engine/coglossa/store/migrations/versions/0001_initial_schema.py`. It is edited in place, because no database outside development exists. Developers drop their local database once.
  - the spec, the follow-up docs, `README.md`, `Makefile` and `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `main` at the start of M4b.
- Produces, for every later task:
  - **Names:** a case-preserving substitution, so `bible → guide`, `Bible → Guide`, `BIBLE → GUIDE`. Examples:
    - `Guide`, `GuideVersion`, `GuideView`, `GuideCheckpoint`
    - `run_guide`, `approve_guide`, `prior_guide`, `project_guide`, `save_project_guide`, `add_guide_entry`
    - `RunState.BUILDING_GUIDE` / `"building_guide"`, `AWAITING_GUIDE_APPROVAL` / `"awaiting_guide_approval"`
  - **Config:** `models.profiler_guide`.
  - **API:**
    - routes: `/api/projects/{project_id}/guide`, `/api/projects/{project_id}/guide/versions`, `/api/runs/{run_id}/guide`, `/api/runs/{run_id}/guide/approve` and `/api/runs/{run_id}/spans/{span_id}/guide`
    - the request field `use_project_guide`.
  - **CLI:** `coglossa guide`, `coglossa approve-guide`, and the flags `--guide`, `--approved-guide` and `--use-project-guide`. Runs write `guide.json`.
  - **Database:** the table `guide_version` and the column `run.guide_version_id`.
  - **Prompts:** the ids `guide` and `guide_merge`, and the input field `guide`.

### Task 1: One project guide, a checkpoint approval that works with it, clean guide text, and a test marker for flags

The web app's guide screens need one answer to "what is the project guide?". M4a gave two answers, recorded in the M4b carry-over list:
- **The M4a editor answer:** `catalog.project_guide`, the save guard and add-to-guide use "the latest version of any status". That includes engine drafts of runs that are still waiting, failed or were cancelled.
- **The worker's answer:** `runs.prior_guide` (M3), which never uses those drafts.

**Consequence in M4a:** as soon as a second run in a project drafts a guide, a run waiting at the checkpoint can never be approved without a body (409).

**Fix:** "the project guide" becomes `runs.prior_guide` everywhere.

Two smaller items ride along:
- Guide bodies sent by people go through `clean_text`. M4a did this for review text but not for guides.
- The synthetic model responder gains a marker that produces a flag. Without it, the Playwright journey (Task 12) has no flag to resolve.

**Files:**
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/store/runs.py` (`prior_guide` docstring, `FINISHED_STATES`)
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/catalog.py` (`_projects_query`, `project_guide`, `save_project_guide`)
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/service.py` (`approve_guide`)
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/review.py` (`add_guide_entry`: its base)
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/text.py` (`clean_guide`)
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/testing/synthetic.py` (`_judge`: the `MISTRANSLATED` marker)
- Test: `engine/tests/test_guides.py`, `engine/tests/test_service.py`, `engine/tests/test_text.py`, `engine/tests/testing/test_synthetic.py`, `api/tests/test_guides.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (M3/M4a, unchanged):
  - `runs.prior_guide(s, project_id) -> GuideVersion | None`
  - `runs.latest_guide(s, project_id, *, approved_only)`
  - `runs.lock_project`
  - `coglossa.text.clean_text(text) -> str`
  - `service.ConflictError`, `service.InvalidRequestError`
- Produces:
  - `coglossa.text.clean_guide(guide: Guide) -> Guide`: every string field passed through `clean_text`.
  - `runs.FINISHED_STATES: tuple[str, str]`: the values of `complete` and `complete_with_errors` (the old private `_FINISHED`).
  - **The project guide:** `runs.prior_guide` defines it everywhere: `GET /projects/{id}/guide` (no version), `ProjectInfo.guide_version`, the save guard, the checkpoint-approval guard and the base of add-to-guide.
  - **Checkpoint approval:** refused (409) only when the project guide's version is **greater** than the base. The base is `base_version` with an edited guide, else the run's draft version.
  - **Synthetic judge:** any span whose translation contains `MISTRANSLATED` gets one critical `accuracy/mistranslation` error with excerpt `MISTRANSLATED`. Aggregation turns that into an open `low_meaning` flag.

**The rules:**
- **The project guide** is the latest approved version (people's saves and approved checkpoints). Without one, it is the latest version whose run finished. Drafts of runs that are waiting, running, failed or cancelled are never the project guide. They stay in the version history (`GET /projects/{id}/guide/versions`) and stay readable by number.
- **Saving the project guide:** `base_version` must equal the project guide's version (`None` when the project has none).
- **Approving at the checkpoint:**
  - The editor loads either the run's draft or the project guide.
  - A save that lands in between (a newer project guide) refuses the approval, so nobody's saved edits disappear.
  - Approving a draft that is newer than the project guide is the normal case and is allowed.
- **Text people send is cleaned:** NUL and other XML-illegal characters are dropped, and vertical tabs and form feeds become line breaks. This applies to every string field of a guide saved or approved with a body.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Add to `engine/tests/test_text.py`:

```python
from coglossa.schema import Guide, Entity, GlossaryEntry
from coglossa.text import clean_guide


def test_clean_guide_cleans_every_text_field():
    dirty = Guide(
        summary="A\x00 cat\x0csleeps.",
        style_guide="Plain\x01.",
        glossary=[GlossaryEntry(term="ch\x00at", rendering="cat\x0b", notes="n\x02", locked=True)],
        entities=[Entity(name="Ma\x00rie", kind="person", rendering="Marie\x0c", gender=None)],
        cultural_references=["Paris\x00"],
    )
    clean = clean_guide(dirty)
    assert clean.summary == "A cat\nsleeps."
    assert clean.style_guide == "Plain."
    assert clean.glossary[0].model_dump() == {
        "term": "chat",
        "rendering": "cat\n",
        "notes": "n",
        "locked": True,
    }
    assert (clean.entities[0].name, clean.entities[0].rendering) == ("Marie", "Marie\n")
    assert clean.entities[0].gender is None
    assert clean.cultural_references == ["Paris"]
    assert clean_guide(clean) == clean
```

Add to `engine/tests/testing/test_synthetic.py`:

```python
from coglossa.schema import JudgeOutput


def test_judge_marks_the_mistranslated_marker():
    out = synthetic_response(
        {
            "task": "judge",
            "spans": [
                {"start": "s1", "end": "s1", "text": "[en] Fine."},
                {"start": "s2", "end": "s2", "text": "[en] It is MISTRANSLATED here."},
            ],
        }
    )
    judged = JudgeOutput.model_validate(out)
    assert judged.spans[0].errors == []
    [error] = judged.spans[1].errors
    assert (error.category.value, error.severity.value, error.excerpt) == (
        "accuracy/mistranslation",
        "critical",
        "MISTRANSLATED",
    )
    assert error.explanation == "Synthetic critical error: the text is marked MISTRANSLATED."
```

Add to `engine/tests/test_guides.py`:

```python
async def test_drafts_of_unfinished_runs_are_not_the_project_guide(db):
    waiting = await seed_run(db, state=RunState.AWAITING_GUIDE_APPROVAL)
    [draft] = await catalog.guide_versions(db, waiting.project_id)
    assert (draft.version, draft.status) == (1, "draft")
    with pytest.raises(NotFoundError, match="has no guide yet"):
        await catalog.project_guide(db, waiting.project_id)
    assert (await catalog.get_project(db, waiting.project_id)).guide_version is None
    assert (await catalog.project_guide(db, waiting.project_id, version=1)).info.version == 1
    with pytest.raises(service.ConflictError, match="now version none, you edited version 1"):
        await catalog.save_project_guide(db, waiting.project_id, LOCKED, base_version=1)
    saved = await catalog.save_project_guide(db, waiting.project_id, LOCKED, base_version=None)
    assert saved.info.version == 2
    assert (await catalog.get_project(db, waiting.project_id)).guide_version == 2


async def test_a_finished_runs_guide_is_the_project_guide_until_one_is_approved(db):
    seeded = await seed_run(db)  # complete; its guide v1 is an engine draft
    assert (await catalog.project_guide(db, seeded.project_id)).info.version == 1
    assert (await catalog.get_project(db, seeded.project_id)).guide_version == 1
    for state in (RunState.FAILED, RunState.CANCELLED, RunState.TRANSLATING):
        await seed_run(db, project="demo", state=state)  # seed_run's default project
    versions = await catalog.guide_versions(db, seeded.project_id)
    assert [v.version for v in versions] == [4, 3, 2, 1]
    assert (await catalog.project_guide(db, seeded.project_id)).info.version == 1
    assert (await catalog.get_project(db, seeded.project_id)).guide_version == 1


async def test_saved_guides_are_cleaned(db):
    seeded = await seed_run(db)
    dirty = LOCKED.model_copy(update={"summary": "Rain\x00 falls.\x0cEnd."})
    saved = await catalog.save_project_guide(db, seeded.project_id, dirty, base_version=1)
    assert saved.guide.summary == "Rain falls.\nEnd."
```

`seed_run(db, project="demo", ...)` reuses the project of that name (`runs.get_or_create_project`) and gives every seeded run its own guide version, so the three extra runs add drafts v2–v4 from runs that are failed, cancelled or still translating.

Add to `engine/tests/test_service.py`, next to `test_approve_refuses_when_the_project_guide_changed`. `_awaiting(env, tmp_path)` is the existing helper that submits a document to project `"novel"` and gives the run a draft guide at the checkpoint, so two calls make two waiting runs in one project:

```python
async def test_another_runs_draft_does_not_block_approval(env, tmp_path):
    db, _, queue, _, _ = env
    first = await _awaiting(env, tmp_path)
    second = await _awaiting(env, tmp_path)  # a newer draft (v2) in the same project
    assert await service.approve_guide(db, queue, first) == 3  # M4a refused this: v2 was "latest"
    with pytest.raises(service.ConflictError, match="now version 3"):
        await service.approve_guide(db, queue, second)  # a person approved v3 after its draft


async def test_approved_guides_are_cleaned(env, tmp_path):
    db, _, queue, _, _ = env
    run_id = await _awaiting(env, tmp_path)
    dirty = Guide(summary="s\x00", style_guide="house\x0c")
    await service.approve_guide(db, queue, run_id, dirty)
    async with db.session() as s:
        run = await runs.get_run(s, run_id)
        version = await runs.get_guide_version(s, run.guide_version_id)
    assert Guide.model_validate(version.content) == Guide(summary="s", style_guide="house\n")
```

```python
def clean_guide(guide: "Guide") -> "Guide":
    """`guide` with every text field cleaned: people paste guides from documents too."""
    return type(guide).model_validate(_clean(guide.model_dump()))


def _clean(value: object) -> object:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return clean_text(value)
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_clean(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: _clean(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    return value
```

```python
async def prior_guide(s: AsyncSession, project_id: uuid.UUID) -> GuideVersion | None:
    """The project guide: what the guide editor shows, what saves and checkpoint approvals are
    measured against, and what a new run of the project builds on (documents in one project
    share a guide, spec §5). It is the latest approved version; without one, the latest version
    whose run finished (complete or complete_with_errors). Drafts of runs that are waiting,
    running, failed or cancelled are never the project guide, and nothing here locks an entry:
    locks come only from people."""
```

```python
    finished = (
```

```python
MISTRANSLATED = "MISTRANSLATED"  # a span whose translation contains it gets a critical error


def _judge(p: Payload) -> Payload:
    def errors(text: str) -> list[Payload]:
        if MISTRANSLATED not in text:
            return []
        return [
            {
                "category": "accuracy/mistranslation",
                "severity": "critical",
                "excerpt": MISTRANSLATED,
                "explanation": f"Synthetic critical error: the text is marked {MISTRANSLATED}.",
            }
        ]

    return {
        "spans": [
            {"start": s["start"], "end": s["end"], "errors": errors(s["text"])}
            for s in p["spans"]
        ]
    }
```

### Task 2: The API serves the web app

Spec §2.1 and §10: the `api` image serves the SPA's static files, so the customer runs one web endpoint.

**What changes:**
- **`api.web_dir`:** a new config setting naming the built app's directory (`web/dist`). When it is set, `GET /` and every path outside `/api` return `index.html`, so client-side routes survive a reload. Built assets come from `/assets/` with long-lived caching.
- **Missing or empty `web_dir`:** `coglossa-api` refuses to start, with one line, when `web_dir` is set but has no `index.html`.
- **Auth test:** the every-route-needs-auth test now walks the real routes (`fastapi.routing.iter_route_contexts`) instead of the OpenAPI paths. This is the M4b carry-over item. The SPA's files are deliberately outside auth: they contain no data, and a trusted-header proxy covers them anyway.
- **README:** the REST section says that `base_version` on an approval needs an edited guide (carry-over).

**Files:**
- Modify: `engine/coglossa/config.py` (`ApiConfig.web_dir`, resolved against the config file like `prompts_dir`)
- Modify: `engine/coglossa.example.yaml` (the `api:` block)
- Create: `api/coglossa_api/web.py`
- Modify: `api/coglossa_api/app.py` (mount the web app last)
- Modify: `api/coglossa_api/main.py` (refuse a `web_dir` without `index.html`)
- Modify: `api/tests/test_app.py` (`_protected_routes`)
- Create: `api/tests/test_web.py`
- Modify: `engine/tests/test_config_m4.py`, `api/tests/test_main.py`
- Modify: `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `ApiConfig` (Task 1 of M4a) and `load_config`, which already resolves `prompts_dir` against the config file's directory.
  - `create_app(cfg, config_path, *, open_queue=...)`.
  - Test fixtures `make_api`, `serve`, `write_config` (`api/tests/conftest.py`).
- Produces:
  - `ApiConfig.web_dir: Path | None = None` (absolute after `load_config`)
  - `coglossa_api.web.mount_web(app: FastAPI, web_dir: Path) -> None`
  - `coglossa_api.web.missing_index(web_dir: Path) -> str | None` (the refusal message, or `None` when the app is there)
  - **Behaviour:**
    - `GET /`, `GET /projects/<id>` and so on return `index.html` with `Cache-Control: no-cache`.
    - `GET /assets/<file>` returns the file with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`.
    - A file at the top of `web_dir` (`/favicon.svg`) is served as itself.
    - Unknown `/api/...` paths stay JSON 404.
    - A path that escapes `web_dir` (`/../x`) never serves a file outside it.

```python
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from coglossa_api.web import missing_index

INDEX = "<!doctype html><title>Coglossa</title><div id=root></div>"


@pytest.fixture
def web_dir(tmp_path) -> Path:
    root = tmp_path / "dist"
    (root / "assets").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "index.html").write_text(INDEX, encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "assets" / "app-1a2b.js").write_text("console.log(1)", encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "favicon.svg").write_text("<svg/>", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "secret.txt").write_text("no", encoding="utf-8")
    return root


async def test_the_app_and_its_routes_return_index(make_api, web_dir):
    async with make_api(api={"web_dir": str(web_dir)}) as api:
        for path in ("/", "/projects/abc", "/runs/1/review?flagged=true"):
            response = await api.client.get(path)
            assert response.status_code == 200, path
            assert response.text == INDEX
            assert response.headers["content-type"].startswith("text/html")
            assert response.headers["cache-control"] == "no-cache"


async def test_assets_are_cached_and_top_level_files_served(make_api, web_dir):
    async with make_api(api={"web_dir": str(web_dir)}) as api:
        asset = await api.client.get("/assets/app-1a2b.js")
        assert asset.status_code == 200 and asset.text == "console.log(1)"
        assert asset.headers["cache-control"] == "public, max-age=31536000, immutable"
        assert (await api.client.get("/assets/missing.js")).status_code == 404
        icon = await api.client.get("/favicon.svg")
        assert icon.status_code == 200 and icon.text == "<svg/>"


async def test_api_paths_stay_json_and_nothing_escapes(make_api, web_dir):
    async with make_api(api={"web_dir": str(web_dir)}) as api:
        unknown = await api.client.get("/api/nope")
        assert unknown.status_code == 404 and unknown.json() == {"detail": "Not Found"}
        assert (await api.client.get("/api/health")).json()["status"] == "ok"
        for path in ("/../secret.txt", "/%2e%2e/secret.txt", "/assets/../../secret.txt"):
            response = await api.client.get(path)
            assert "no" != response.text, path
        assert (await api.client.post("/projects/abc")).status_code == 405


async def test_the_web_app_needs_no_user(make_api, web_dir):
    async with make_api(api={"web_dir": str(web_dir)}, auth={"mode": "trusted_header"}) as api:
        assert (await api.client.get("/")).status_code == 200
        assert (await api.client.get("/api/me")).status_code == 401


async def test_without_web_dir_there_is_no_web_app(make_api):
    async with make_api() as api:
        assert (await api.client.get("/")).status_code == 404


def test_missing_index(tmp_path):
    assert missing_index(tmp_path / "nowhere") == (
        f"api.web_dir {tmp_path / 'nowhere'} has no index.html; build the web app "
        "(cd web && pnpm build) or remove api.web_dir"
    )
    (tmp_path / "index.html").write_text(INDEX, encoding="utf-8")
    assert missing_index(tmp_path) is None
```

In `api/tests/test_app.py`, replace `_protected_routes` so it walks the real routes:

```python
from fastapi.routing import iter_route_contexts


def _protected_routes() -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Every /api route of the app, found by walking its routers (not its OpenAPI document,
    which leaves out routes hidden from the schema), except health."""
    app = create_app(CoglossaConfig.model_validate(SEED_CONFIG), Path("coglossa.yaml"))
    routes = {
        (method, route.path)
        for route in iter_route_contexts(app.routes)
        if route.path.startswith("/api/")
        for method in route.methods or ()
        if method != "HEAD"
    }
    return sorted(routes - {("GET", "/api/health")})
```

`test_every_route_is_listed` stays. It asserts `("GET", "/api/openapi.json")` is listed, which now comes from the walk itself.

Create `api/coglossa_api/web.py`:

```python
"""Serving the web app (spec §2.1): the API hands out the SPA's built files, so a site runs one
web endpoint. Every path outside /api returns index.html and the app routes on the client;
built assets carry content hashes, so browsers may keep them for a year. The files hold no
data, so they are served without the trusted header (the proxy in front adds it anyway)."""

from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from starlette.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from starlette.types import Scope

IMMUTABLE = "public, max-age=31536000, immutable"


class _Assets(StaticFiles):
    async def get_response(self, path: str, scope: Scope):  # type: ignore[override]
        response = await super().get_response(path, scope)
        if response.status_code == 200:
            response.headers["Cache-Control"] = IMMUTABLE
        return response


def missing_index(web_dir: Path) -> str | None:
    if (web_dir / "index.html").is_file():
        return None
    return (
        f"api.web_dir {web_dir} has no index.html; build the web app (cd web && pnpm build) "
        "or remove api.web_dir"
    )


def mount_web(app: FastAPI, web_dir: Path) -> None:
    """Mount last, after every /api router: the catch-all must not shadow them."""
    root = web_dir.resolve()
    index = root / "index.html"
    if (root / "assets").is_dir():
        app.mount("/assets", _Assets(directory=root / "assets"), name="assets")

    @app.get("/{path:path}", include_in_schema=False)
    async def web_app(path: str, request: Request) -> FileResponse:
        if path == "api" or path.startswith("api/"):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404)
        candidate = (root / path).resolve()
        if path and candidate.is_file() and candidate.is_relative_to(root):
            return FileResponse(candidate)
        return FileResponse(index, headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})
```

**Why `/assets/missing.js` returns 404:** the `/assets` mount answers it, not the catch-all. If `StaticFiles` returns a 404 that the app's error handlers turn into `{"detail": "Not Found"}`, that is fine; the test only checks the status.

**Path escapes:**
- `/../secret.txt` and `/%2e%2e/secret.txt` are normalized by the HTTP client or Starlette before routing, or rejected by `is_relative_to`. Either way the response is never the secret.
- `/assets/../../secret.txt` is handled by `StaticFiles`, which refuses to leave its directory.

`api/coglossa_api/app.py`: at the end of `create_app`, after `app.include_router(protected)`:

```python
    if cfg.api.web_dir is not None:
        mount_web(app, cfg.api.web_dir)
    return app
```

with `from coglossa_api.web import mount_web` among the `coglossa_api` imports.

`api/coglossa_api/main.py`, after `build_storage(cfg.storage).check_writable()` inside the `try`:

```python
        if cfg.api.web_dir is not None and (problem := missing_index(cfg.api.web_dir)):
            raise ConfigError(problem)
```

with `from coglossa_api.web import missing_index`.

`ConfigError(message)` takes the one-line message, as the other refusals do. If its constructor differs, follow how `ensure_migrated` raises it.

2. **`base_version`.** Where the README describes approving a guide, extend the sentence to: "…approve with the edited guide and the `base_version` it started from (the run's draft or the project guide). `base_version` without a `guide` is refused (422), and a project guide saved after that base refuses the approval (409)."

### Task 3: The web project: tooling, generated API types, the typed client, the app shell, Make and CI

This task creates `web/`, a TypeScript React single-page app built with Vite and managed with pnpm (spec §2.1, §2.2). It leaves the app with:
- **The app shell:** a top bar with Projects and System links, the signed-in user, and a "page not found" page.
- **API access:** the typed API client generated from `schema/openapi.json`, plus error handling.
- **The test harness** that every later task uses: Vitest, Testing Library and msw.
- **Build wiring:** Make targets and a CI job with the 95% coverage gate.

Every setting in this task comes from a spike that ran with these exact versions. The **Toolchain notes** below explain the parts that look unusual. Keep them.

**Toolchain notes:**
1. **pnpm 10 install scripts.** pnpm 10 skips dependency install scripts unless they are listed, so `esbuild` and `msw` go in `pnpm.onlyBuiltDependencies`.
2. **Late-bound fetch.** `openapi-fetch` captures `globalThis.fetch` when `createClient` runs, before msw patches it in tests. The client therefore gets a late-bound `fetch` wrapper.
3. **Absolute base URL.** Node's fetch (used under jsdom) rejects relative URLs, so the client's `baseUrl` is `location.origin`. In a browser that is the page's own origin, so the app still calls the server it was loaded from. msw handlers match `*/api/...`.
4. **jsdom AbortController.** Vitest's jsdom environment replaces `AbortController` with jsdom's. react-router then builds a Node `Request` with a jsdom signal, which throws, and navigation silently does nothing. `src/test/jsdom-env.ts` keeps Node's `AbortController`.
5. **Uploads in tests.** jsdom's `FormData` and `File` are not understood by Node's fetch. Only the upload test swaps in Node's classes (`src/test/node-web.ts`); the app code needs nothing special.
6. **Generated upload type.** The generated type for the upload body says `file: string` and makes `use_project_guide` required. The upload call casts the `File` and always sends `use_project_guide`.
7. **Playwright listening address.** Playwright's `webServer` must listen on `127.0.0.1` explicitly; on some machines `localhost` resolves to `::1` and the wait times out.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - project config: `web/package.json`, `web/pnpm-lock.yaml` (generated by `pnpm install`), `web/tsconfig.json`, `web/vite.config.ts`, `web/eslint.config.js`, `web/.prettierrc.json`, `web/.prettierignore`, `web/.gitignore`, `web/index.html`
  - app entry and layout: `web/src/main.tsx`, `web/src/routes.tsx`, `web/src/Providers.tsx`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
  - API layer: `web/src/api/schema.d.ts` (generated by `pnpm gen:api`), `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/api/system.ts`
  - shared pieces: `web/src/lib/format.ts`
  - components: `web/src/components/Layout.tsx`, `web/src/components/ErrorMessage.tsx`, `web/src/components/Loading.tsx`
  - pages: `web/src/pages/NotFound.tsx`, `web/src/pages/RouteError.tsx`
  - test harness: `web/src/test/jsdom-env.ts`, `web/src/test/setup.ts`, `web/src/test/server.ts`, `web/src/test/node-web.ts`, `web/src/test/render.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`
  - tests: `web/src/api/client.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/format.test.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout.test.tsx`, `web/src/Providers.test.tsx`
- Modify: `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.gitignore` (root, if it ignores nothing under `web/` already)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `schema/openapi.json` (M4a and Tasks 1–2 of this plan)
  - `GET /api/me` → `{user: string | null}`
- Produces, for every later task:
  - `client` (`web/src/api/client.ts`): the typed `openapi-fetch` client. Call it as `client.GET("/api/...", {params: {path, query}})` or `client.POST(..., {body})`.
  - `unwrap<T>(call): Promise<T>`: awaits an `openapi-fetch` call and returns `data`. It throws `ApiError` on a non-2xx answer or a network failure.
  - `class ApiError extends Error { status: number }`: `status` is 0 for a network failure. `message` is the server's one-line `detail`, or the first validation message as `"<field>: <msg>"`.
  - `NETWORK_ERROR`: the message used for a network failure.
  - `errorText(status, body): string`
  - URL builders, for the few URLs a browser loads directly:
    - `eventsUrl(runId)` → `/api/runs/<id>/events`
    - `exportUrl(runId, kind, comments?)` → `/api/runs/<id>/exports/<kind>`, plus `?comments=false` only when `comments === false`
  - `web/src/api/types.ts`: named aliases of every schema the app uses:
    - **projects and runs:** `ProjectInfo`, `CreateProject`, `DocumentSummary`, `RunSummary`, `RunRequest`, `RunDetail`, `RunProgress`, `RunState`, `ActionResult`, `DocumentProfile`, `PipelineProfile`
    - **guide:** `Guide`, `GuideView`, `GuideVersionInfo`, `GlossaryEntry`, `Entity`
    - **review:** `SpanPage`, `ReviewSpan`, `ReviewFlag`, `SpanDetail`, `SpanScore`, `Candidate`, `JudgeError`, `RevisionEntry`, `Flag`, `FlagCode`, `FlagStatus`, `Reliability`, `ExportKind`
    - **system:** `DoctorReport`, `DoctorCheck`, `ConfigView`, `TierTable`
  - `useMe()` (`web/src/api/system.ts`): a TanStack Query hook with key `["me"]`.
  - `makeQueryClient(): QueryClient` and `<Providers client={...}>` (`web/src/Providers.tsx`). Queries are not retried on a 4xx; other failures are retried once.
  - `routes: RouteObject[]` (`web/src/routes.tsx`): later tasks add their pages as children of the layout route.
  - `<Layout/>`, `<ErrorMessage error={unknown}/>` (`role="alert"`) and `<Loading/>` (`role="status"`, text "Loading…").
  - `web/src/lib/format.ts`:
    - `TERMINAL_STATES`, `isTerminal(state)`, `stateLabel(state)`
    - `formatDate(iso)`, `formatScore(value)`, `languagePair(source, target)`
    - `LANGUAGE_CODE` (a `RegExp` equal to the API's pattern), `isLanguageCode(text)`
    - `profileLabel(profile)`
  - Test harness:
    - `renderApp(path)` (`web/src/test/render.tsx`) → `{router, user, queryClient}`
    - `server` (`web/src/test/server.ts`), whose default handler answers `GET /api/me` with `{user: null}`
    - `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`: typed sample objects that later tasks extend
  - Make targets:
    - `make test-web`, which `make test` now includes
    - `make e2e` (Task 12 adds the specs)
    - `make lint` and `make fmt` now include the web project
    - `make schema` also regenerates `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`

- [ ] **Step 2: Create the test harness**

`web/src/test/jsdom-env.ts`:

```ts
import type { Environment } from "vitest/environments";
import { builtinEnvironments } from "vitest/environments";

// Vitest's jsdom environment replaces AbortController with jsdom's. react-router builds a Node
// (undici) Request with that signal, which rejects it: navigations then fail silently. Keep
// Node's AbortController (AbortSignal stays Node's too).
const env: Environment = {
  ...builtinEnvironments.jsdom,
  name: "jsdom-node-abort",
  async setup(global, options) {
    const NodeAbortController = global.AbortController;
    const result = await builtinEnvironments.jsdom.setup(global, options);
    global.AbortController = NodeAbortController;
    return result;
  },
};
export default env;
```

```ts
// jsdom brings its own FormData and File, which Node's fetch cannot read. The upload test
// swaps in Node's classes with vi.stubGlobal; nothing else needs them.
import { File as NodeFile } from "node:buffer";

export async function nodeFormData(): Promise<typeof FormData> {
  const parsed = await new Response("a=1", {
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
  }).formData();
  return parsed.constructor as typeof FormData;
}

export const NodeFileClass = NodeFile as unknown as typeof File;
```

`web/src/test/render.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from "react-router";
import { makeQueryClient, Providers } from "../Providers";
import { routes } from "../routes";

/** Render the whole app at `path`, as a browser would after loading that URL. */
export function renderApp(path: string) {
  const router = createMemoryRouter(routes, { initialEntries: [path] });
  const queryClient = makeQueryClient({ retry: false });
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(
    <Providers client={queryClient}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </Providers>,
  );
  return { router, user, queryClient };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing tests**

`web/src/api/client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { server } from "../test/server";
import { ApiError, client, errorText, eventsUrl, exportUrl, NETWORK_ERROR, unwrap } from "./client";

describe("unwrap", () => {
  it("returns the data of a 2xx answer", async () => {
    server.use(http.get("*/api/me", () => HttpResponse.json({ user: "ana" })));
    expect(await unwrap(client.GET("/api/me"))).toEqual({ user: "ana" });
  });

  it("throws the server's one-line detail", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("*/api/me", () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ detail: "missing X-Remote-User header" }, { status: 401 }),
      ),
    );
    const error = await unwrap(client.GET("/api/me")).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ status: 401, message: "missing X-Remote-User header" });
  });

  it("throws a network error when the server cannot be reached", async () => {
    server.use(http.get("*/api/me", () => HttpResponse.error()));
    await expect(unwrap(client.GET("/api/me"))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 0,
      message: NETWORK_ERROR,
    });
  });
});

describe("errorText", () => {
  it("reads FastAPI validation errors", () => {
    const body = { detail: [{ loc: ["body", "target_lang"], msg: "String should match pattern" }] };
    expect(errorText(422, body)).toBe("target_lang: String should match pattern");
    expect(errorText(422, { detail: [{ loc: ["body"], msg: "Field required" }] })).toBe(
      "Field required",
    );
  });

  it("explains an answer without a detail", () => {
    expect(errorText(502, undefined)).toBe("The server answered 502 without an explanation.");
    expect(errorText(500, { detail: "" })).toBe("The server answered 500 without an explanation.");
    expect(errorText(500, { detail: [] })).toBe("The server answered 500 without an explanation.");
  });
});

describe("urls", () => {
  it("builds the URLs a browser loads directly", () => {
    expect(eventsUrl("r 1")).toBe("/api/runs/r%201/events");
    expect(exportUrl("r1", "html")).toBe("/api/runs/r1/exports/html");
    expect(exportUrl("r1", "bilingual-docx", false)).toBe(
      "/api/runs/r1/exports/bilingual-docx?comments=false",
    );
    expect(exportUrl("r1", "bilingual-docx", true)).toBe("/api/runs/r1/exports/bilingual-docx");
  });
});
```

```ts
import {
  formatDate,
  formatScore,
  isLanguageCode,
  isTerminal,
  languagePair,
  profileLabel,
  stateLabel,
} from "./format";

it("labels run states for people", () => {

  expect(stateLabel("complete_with_errors")).toBe("Complete with errors");
  expect(isTerminal("failed")).toBe(true);
  expect(isTerminal("sweeping")).toBe(false);
});

it("formats scores, dates, language pairs and profiles", () => {
  expect(formatScore(null)).toBe("—");
  expect(formatScore(undefined)).toBe("—");
  expect(formatScore(71.6)).toBe("72");
  expect(formatDate(null)).toBe("—");
  expect(formatDate("2026-10-01T09:00:00Z")).toMatch(/2026/);
  expect(languagePair("fr", "en")).toBe("fr → en");
  expect(languagePair(null, "en")).toBe("auto-detect → en");
  expect(profileLabel(null)).toBe("Automatic");
  expect(profileLabel("long-form")).toBe("Long-form");
});

it("checks language codes like the API does", () => {
  for (const ok of ["en", "pt-BR", "zh-Hant-TW", "es_419", "fil"]) {
    expect(isLanguageCode(ok)).toBe(true);
  }
  for (const bad of ["", "e", "en--US", "1234", "english language"]) {
    expect(isLanguageCode(bad)).toBe(false);
  }
});
```

`web/src/Providers.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ApiError } from "./api/client";
import { makeQueryClient } from "./Providers";

it("retries a failed query once, but never a 4xx", () => {
  const retry = makeQueryClient().getDefaultOptions().queries?.retry as (
    count: number,
    error: unknown,
  ) => boolean;
  expect(retry(0, new ApiError(404, "gone"))).toBe(false);
  expect(retry(0, new ApiError(503, "down"))).toBe(true);
  expect(retry(0, new ApiError(0, "network"))).toBe(true);
  expect(retry(1, new ApiError(503, "down"))).toBe(false);
  expect(makeQueryClient({ retry: false }).getDefaultOptions().queries?.retry).toBe(false);
});
```

```tsx
it("shows the navigation and the signed-in user", async () => {

  expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("kaput");
```

Run: `cd web && pnpm exec vitest run`
Expected: FAIL. Imports of `./client`, `./format`, `./Providers`, `../routes` cannot be resolved.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the API layer**

`web/src/api/client.ts`:

```ts
import createClient from "openapi-fetch";
import type { paths } from "./schema";
import type { ExportKind } from "./types";

// openapi-fetch keeps the fetch it is given; a late-bound wrapper lets tests (msw) patch fetch.
const lateFetch: typeof fetch = (input, init) => globalThis.fetch(input, init);

// The app always talks to the server it was loaded from. location.origin rather than "" because
// Node's fetch (tests) refuses relative URLs; in a browser the two are the same.
export const client = createClient<paths>({ baseUrl: location.origin, fetch: lateFetch });

export const NETWORK_ERROR =
  "The Coglossa server could not be reached; check that it is running and try again.";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: number,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

type ValidationItem = { loc?: (string | number)[]; msg?: string };
const PLACES = new Set(["body", "query", "path"]);

/** The one line to show for a failed request: the API's `detail` (spec: errors are one line). */
export function errorText(status: number, body: unknown): string {
  const detail = (body as { detail?: unknown } | null | undefined)?.detail;
  if (typeof detail === "string" && detail) return detail;
  if (Array.isArray(detail) && detail.length > 0) {
    const first = detail[0] as ValidationItem;
    const field = (first.loc ?? []).filter((part) => !PLACES.has(String(part))).join(".");
    return field ? `${field}: ${first.msg}` : String(first.msg);
  }
  return `The server answered ${status} without an explanation.`;
}

/** The data of an API call, or an ApiError with the message to show. */
export async function unwrap<T>(
  call: Promise<{ data?: T; error?: unknown; response: Response }>,
): Promise<T> {
  let result: { data?: T; error?: unknown; response: Response };
  try {
    result = await call;
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(0, NETWORK_ERROR);
  }
  if (!result.response.ok) {
    throw new ApiError(result.response.status, errorText(result.response.status, result.error));
  }
  return result.data as T;
}

/** URLs the browser loads itself (an EventSource, a download link) rather than through fetch. */
export function eventsUrl(runId: string): string {
  return `/api/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}/events`;
}

export function exportUrl(runId: string, kind: ExportKind, comments?: boolean): string {
  const base = `/api/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}/exports/${kind}`;
  return comments === false ? `${base}?comments=false` : base;
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { ApiError } from "./api/client";

/** A 4xx will not change on a retry; a network error or 5xx might, once. */
function retryOnce(count: number, error: unknown): boolean {
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status >= 400 && error.status < 500) return false;
  return count < 1;
}

export function makeQueryClient({ retry = true }: { retry?: boolean } = {}): QueryClient {
  return new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: { retry: retry ? retryOnce : false, refetchOnWindowFocus: false },
      mutations: { retry: false },
    },
  });
}

export function Providers({ client, children }: { client: QueryClient; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}
```

`web/src/lib/format.ts`:

```ts
import type { PipelineProfile, RunState } from "../api/types";

export const TERMINAL_STATES: readonly RunState[] = [
  "complete",
  "complete_with_errors",
  "failed",
  "cancelled",
];

export function isTerminal(state: RunState): boolean {
  return TERMINAL_STATES.includes(state);
}

const STATE_LABELS: Record<RunState, string> = {
  created: "Queued",
  ingesting: "Reading the document",
  profiling: "Profiling",
  building_guide: "Building the guide",
  awaiting_guide_approval: "Waiting for guide approval",
  translating: "Translating",
  sweeping: "Checking consistency",
  complete: "Complete",
  complete_with_errors: "Complete with errors",
  failed: "Failed",
  cancelled: "Cancelled",
};

export function stateLabel(state: RunState): string {
  return STATE_LABELS[state];
}

export function formatScore(value: number | null | undefined): string {
  return value === null || value === undefined ? "—" : Math.round(value).toString();
}

export function formatDate(iso: string | null): string {
  return iso === null ? "—" : new Date(iso).toLocaleString();
}

export function languagePair(source: string | null, target: string): string {
  return `${source ?? "auto-detect"} → ${target}`;
}

export function profileLabel(profile: PipelineProfile | null): string {
  if (profile === null) return "Automatic";
  return profile === "long-form" ? "Long-form" : profile[0]!.toUpperCase() + profile.slice(1);
}

// The API's LANGUAGE_CODE_PATTERN (coglossa.schema.views): ISO 639 code, optional subtags.
export const LANGUAGE_CODE = /^[A-Za-z]{2,3}(?:[-_][A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$/;

export function isLanguageCode(text: string): boolean {
  return text.length <= 35 && LANGUAGE_CODE.test(text);
}
```

```tsx
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";

export function messageOf(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiError || error instanceof Error) return error.message;
  return "Something went wrong.";
}

export function ErrorMessage({ error }: { error: unknown }) {
  return (
    <p role="alert" className="error">
      {messageOf(error)}
    </p>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";

/** Shown when a page throws while rendering: the app keeps its frame, and the error is named. */
export function RouteError() {
  const error = useRouteError();
  return (
    <section className="app">
      <h1>Something went wrong</h1>
      <ErrorMessage error={error} />
      <p>
        <Link to="/projects">Back to the projects</Link>
      </p>

}
```

```tsx
import { Layout } from "./components/Layout";
import { NotFound } from "./pages/NotFound";
import { RouteError } from "./pages/RouteError";

/** Later tasks add their pages to the layout route's children, before the catch-all. */
export const routes: RouteObject[] = [

    path: "/",
    element: <Layout />,
    errorElement: <RouteError />,
    children: [
```

```tsx
  renderApp("/projects");

it("shows why the list could not load", async () => {

it("checks language codes before sending", async () => {

  await screen.findByText(/No projects yet/);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Name"), "x");
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Target language"), "english");
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Source language"), "fr fr");
  expect(screen.getAllByText("Use a language code such as en, pt-BR or zh-Hant")).toHaveLength(2);
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Create project" })).toBeDisabled();

it("shows the server's refusal", async () => {

  server.use(
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`web/src/api/projects.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { client, unwrap } from "./client";
import type { CreateProject } from "./types";

export const projectKeys = {
  all: ["projects"] as const,
  one: (projectId: string) => ["projects", projectId] as const,
  documents: (projectId: string) => ["projects", projectId, "documents"] as const,
};

export function useProjects() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: projectKeys.all,
    queryFn: () => unwrap(client.GET("/api/projects")),
  });
}

export function useProject(projectId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: projectKeys.one(projectId),
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(client.GET("/api/projects/{project_id}", { params: { path: { project_id: projectId } } })),
  });
}

export function useCreateProject() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: CreateProject) => unwrap(client.POST("/api/projects", { body })),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.all }),
  });
}
```

`web/src/components/LanguageInput.tsx`. The label text sits in its own `<label>` and the hint sits outside it, so the input's accessible name stays "Target language" while the hint shows; the tests find inputs by that name:

```tsx
import { useId } from "react";
import { isLanguageCode } from "../lib/format";

export const LANGUAGE_HINT = "Use a language code such as en, pt-BR or zh-Hant";

type Props = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  required?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
};

export function LanguageInput({ label, value, onChange, required, placeholder }: Props) {
  const id = useId();
  const invalid = value !== "" && !isLanguageCode(value);
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <input
        id={id}
        value={value}
        required={required}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        aria-invalid={invalid}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value.trim())}
        size={12}
      />
      {invalid ? <span className="error"> {LANGUAGE_HINT}</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/** True when the field is acceptable: a valid code, or empty when optional. */
export function languageOk(value: string, required = false): boolean {
  return value === "" ? !required : isLanguageCode(value);
}
```

```tsx
import { formatDate, languagePair, profileLabel } from "../lib/format";

export const PROFILES: PipelineProfile[] = ["short", "standard", "long-form"];

export function ProfileSelect({
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: PipelineProfile | "";
  onChange: (value: PipelineProfile | "") => void;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>Pipeline profile</label>
      <select
        id={id}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value as PipelineProfile | "")}
      >
        <option value="">Automatic (from the document's length)</option>
        {PROFILES.map((p) => (
          <option key={p} value={p}>
            {profileLabel(p)}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

function ProjectTable({ projects }: { projects: ProjectInfo[] }) {
  if (projects.length === 0) {
    return <p className="muted">No projects yet. Create one to translate a document.</p>;
  }
  return (
    <table aria-label="Projects">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>Name</th>
          <th>Languages</th>
          <th>Profile</th>
          <th>Documents</th>
          <th>Runs</th>
          <th>Guide</th>
          <th>Created</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {projects.map((p) => (
          <tr key={p.id}>
            <td>
              <Link to={`/projects/${p.id}`} dir="auto">
                {p.name}
              </Link>
            </td>
            <td>{p.target_lang ? languagePair(p.source_lang, p.target_lang) : "—"}</td>
            <td>{profileLabel(p.pipeline_profile)}</td>
            <td>{p.documents}</td>
            <td>{p.runs}</td>
            <td>{p.guide_version === null ? "—" : `v${p.guide_version}`}</td>
            <td>{formatDate(p.created_at)}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}

function CreateProjectForm() {
  const create = useCreateProject();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [target, setTarget] = useState("");
  const [source, setSource] = useState("");
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<PipelineProfile | "">("");
  const ready = name.trim() !== "" && languageOk(target, true) && languageOk(source);

  function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    create.mutate(
      {
        name: name.trim(),
        target_lang: target,
        source_lang: source || null,
        pipeline_profile: profile || null,
      },
      { onSuccess: (created) => navigate(`/projects/${created.id}`) },
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} aria-label="New project">
      <h2>New project</h2>
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="project-name">Name</label>
        <input
          id="project-name"
          dir="auto"
          value={name}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          required
        />
      </div>
      <LanguageInput label="Target language" value={target} onChange={setTarget} required />
      <LanguageInput
        label="Source language"
        value={source}
        onChange={setSource}
        placeholder="auto-detect"
      />
      <ProfileSelect value={profile} onChange={setProfile} />
      <button type="submit" disabled={!ready || create.isPending}>
        Create project
      </button>
      {create.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={create.error} /> : null}
    </form>
  );
}

export function ProjectsPage() {
  const projects = useProjects();
  return (
    <section>
      <h1>Projects</h1>
      {projects.isPending ? <Loading /> : null}
      {projects.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={projects.error} /> : null}
      {projects.data ? <ProjectTable projects={projects.data} /> : null}
      <CreateProjectForm />
    </section>
  );
}
```

`CreateProject.pipeline_profile` may be generated as `PipelineProfile | null` (optional). `profile || null` fits either way. If `tsc` says `source_lang`/`pipeline_profile` cannot be `null`, the generated type is `?:` without null; use `undefined` instead of `null`, and expect the keys to be absent in the test's `sent` object.

### Task 5: A project: upload a document, its documents and runs, a new run, delete

This task covers spec §8.2: upload, the list of runs per document, and starting a new run. Deleting a project needs its name typed, as the API requires (`DELETE /projects/{id}?confirm=<name>`).

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/api/projects.ts` (documents, upload, new run, delete)
- Create:
  - `web/src/pages/ProjectPage.tsx`
  - `web/src/pages/UploadForm.tsx`
  - `web/src/pages/DocumentList.tsx`
  - `web/src/pages/DeleteProject.tsx`
  - `web/src/components/StateBadge.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/routes.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
- Test:
  - `web/src/pages/ProjectPage.test.tsx`
  - `web/src/api/projects.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–4): `projectKeys`, `useProject`, `LanguageInput`, `languageOk`, `ProfileSelect`, `stateLabel`, `languagePair`, `formatDate`, the fixtures.
- Produces:
  - **Document hooks:** `useDocuments(projectId)`, `useUpload(projectId)`, `useNewRun(projectId)`, `useDeleteProject(projectId)`.
  - **`uploadDocument(projectId, upload: Upload): Promise<RunSummary>`.**
    - `type Upload = { file: File; target_lang: string; source_lang: string | null; pipeline_profile: PipelineProfile | null; checkpoint: boolean | null; use_project_guide: boolean }`.
  - **`<StateBadge state={RunState}/>`:** a `badge` span with `stateLabel(state)`, and the class `state-<state>`.
  - **`ACCEPTED_FILES`:** the extensions the engine reads (`.txt,.md,.markdown,.docx,.html,.htm,.xhtml,.epub,.pdf`).
  - **Routes:** `/projects/:projectId` → `ProjectPage`.
  - **Fixtures:** `documentSummary: DocumentSummary`, whose `runs` is `[runSummary]`.

Append to `web/src/api/projects.ts` (update its imports):

```ts
import type { CreateProject, PipelineProfile, RunRequest, RunSummary } from "./types";

export type Upload = {
  file: File;
  target_lang: string;
  source_lang: string | null;
  pipeline_profile: PipelineProfile | null;
  checkpoint: boolean | null;
  use_project_guide: boolean;
};

export function useDocuments(projectId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: projectKeys.documents(projectId),
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(
        client.GET("/api/projects/{project_id}/documents", {
          params: { path: { project_id: projectId } },
        }),
      ),
  });
}

/** POST the file as multipart form data; fields left null are not sent (the API's defaults). */
export function uploadDocument(projectId: string, upload: Upload): Promise<RunSummary> {
  return unwrap(
    client.POST("/api/projects/{project_id}/runs", {
      params: { path: { project_id: projectId } },
      // The generated type calls the file a string (binary form field); FormData carries it.
      body: { ...upload, file: upload.file as unknown as string },
      bodySerializer(body) {
        const form = new FormData();
        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(body)) {
          if (value === null || value === undefined) continue;
          // typeof, not instanceof Blob: the file may come from another realm (tests).
          if (typeof value === "object") form.append(key, value as Blob);
          else form.append(key, String(value));
        }
        return form; // no Content-Type: the browser adds the multipart boundary
      },
    }),
  );
}

export function useUpload(projectId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (upload: Upload) => uploadDocument(projectId, upload),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.all }),
  });
}

export function useNewRun(projectId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ documentId, body }: { documentId: string; body: RunRequest }) =>
      unwrap(
        client.POST("/api/documents/{document_id}/runs", {
          params: { path: { document_id: documentId } },
          body,
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.one(projectId) }),
  });
}

export function useDeleteProject(projectId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (confirm: string) =>
      unwrap(
        client.DELETE("/api/projects/{project_id}", {
          params: { path: { project_id: projectId }, query: { confirm } },
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.one(projectId) });
      return queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.all });
    },
  });
}
```

**Type adjustments:**
- If the generated `RunRequest` requires keys other than `use_project_guide` (fields with defaults are generated as required), send the minimal body that type-checks. Keep `use_project_guide: true`, and update the test's expected `sent` object to the exact body you send.
- The upload body type comes from `Body_upload_api_projects__project_id__runs_post`. If spreading `upload` into it does not type-check, build the object field by field.

`web/src/pages/UploadForm.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { useUpload } from "../api/projects";
import type { PipelineProfile, ProjectInfo } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { LanguageInput, languageOk } from "../components/LanguageInput";
import { ProfileSelect } from "./ProjectsPage";

export const ACCEPTED_FILES = ".txt,.md,.markdown,.docx,.html,.htm,.xhtml,.epub,.pdf";

export function UploadForm({ project }: { project: ProjectInfo }) {
  const upload = useUpload(project.id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(project.target_lang ?? "");
  const [source, setSource] = useState(project.source_lang ?? "");
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<PipelineProfile | "">(project.pipeline_profile ?? "");
  const [checkpoint, setCheckpoint] = useState(false);
  const [useGuide, setUseGuide] = useState(true);
  const ready = file !== null && languageOk(target, true) && languageOk(source);

  function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (file === null) return;
    upload.mutate(
      {
        file,
        target_lang: target,
        source_lang: source || null,
        pipeline_profile: profile || null,
        checkpoint: checkpoint ? true : null,
        use_project_guide: useGuide,
      },
      { onSuccess: (run) => navigate(`/runs/${run.id}`) },
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} aria-label="Translate a document">
      <h2>Translate a document</h2>
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="document">Document</label>
        <input
          id="document"
          type="file"
          accept={ACCEPTED_FILES}
          onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)}
        />
        <span className="muted"> Text, Markdown, Word, HTML, EPUB or PDF with a text layer.</span>
      </div>
      <LanguageInput label="Target language" value={target} onChange={setTarget} required />
      <LanguageInput
        label="Source language"
        value={source}
        onChange={setSource}
        placeholder="auto-detect"
      />
      <ProfileSelect value={profile} onChange={setProfile} />
      <label>
        <input type="checkbox" checked={checkpoint} onChange={(e) => setCheckpoint(e.target.checked)} />{" "}
        Stop for guide review before translating (otherwise the configured default applies)
      </label>
      <label>
        <input type="checkbox" checked={useGuide} onChange={(e) => setUseGuide(e.target.checked)} />{" "}
        Build on the project guide
      </label>
      <button type="submit" disabled={!ready || upload.isPending}>
        {upload.isPending ? "Uploading…" : "Upload and translate"}
      </button>
      {upload.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={upload.error} /> : null}
    </form>
  );
}
```

Each `<input type="checkbox">` sits inside its `<label>`, so `getByLabelText(/Stop for guide review/)` finds it.

`web/src/pages/DocumentList.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { useNewRun } from "../api/projects";
import type { DocumentSummary } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { StateBadge } from "../components/StateBadge";
import { formatDate, languagePair } from "../lib/format";

export function DocumentList({
  projectId,
  documents,
}: {
  projectId: string;
  documents: DocumentSummary[];
}) {
  const newRun = useNewRun(projectId);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  if (documents.length === 0) return <p className="muted">No documents yet.</p>;
  return (
    <>
      <table aria-label="Documents">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Document</th>
            <th>Runs</th>
            <th />
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {documents.map((doc) => (
            <tr key={doc.id}>
              <td>
                <span dir="auto">{doc.filename}</span>
                <div className="muted">
                  {doc.format.toUpperCase()} · uploaded {formatDate(doc.created_at)}
                </div>
              </td>
              <td>
                <ul className="runs">
                  {doc.runs.map((run) => (
                    <li key={run.id}>
                      <StateBadge state={run.state} /> {languagePair(run.source_lang, run.target_lang)}{" "}
                      · {formatDate(run.created_at)} ·{" "}
                      <Link to={`/runs/${run.id}`}>Open run</Link>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </td>
              <td>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={`Translate ${doc.filename} again`}
                  disabled={newRun.isPending}
                  onClick={() =>
                    newRun.mutate(
                      { documentId: doc.id, body: { use_project_guide: true } },
                      { onSuccess: (run) => navigate(`/runs/${run.id}`) },
                    )
                  }
                >
                  Translate again
                </button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {newRun.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={newRun.error} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/src/pages/DeleteProject.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { useDeleteProject } from "../api/projects";
import type { ProjectInfo } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";

export function DeleteProject({ project }: { project: ProjectInfo }) {
  const remove = useDeleteProject(project.id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState("");
  return (
    <section className="danger-zone">
      <h2>Delete project</h2>
      <p>This deletes every document, run, guide version and review decision in the project.</p>
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="confirm-delete">Type the project name to confirm</label>
        <input id="confirm-delete" dir="auto" value={typed} onChange={(e) => setTyped(e.target.value)} />
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="danger"
        disabled={typed !== project.name || remove.isPending}
        onClick={() => remove.mutate(typed, { onSuccess: () => navigate("/projects") })}
      >
        Delete project
      </button>
      {remove.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={remove.error} /> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useParams } from "react-router";
import { useDocuments, useProject } from "../api/projects";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../components/Loading";
import { languagePair, profileLabel } from "../lib/format";
import { DeleteProject } from "./DeleteProject";
import { DocumentList } from "./DocumentList";
import { UploadForm } from "./UploadForm";

export function ProjectPage() {
  const { projectId = "" } = useParams();
  const project = useProject(projectId);
  const documents = useDocuments(projectId);
  if (project.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (project.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={project.error} />;
  const p = project.data;
  return (
    <section>
      <h1 dir="auto">{p.name}</h1>
      <p className="muted">
        {p.target_lang ? languagePair(p.source_lang, p.target_lang) : "No default languages"} ·{" "}
        {profileLabel(p.pipeline_profile)} profile
      </p>
      <h2>Documents</h2>
      {documents.isPending ? <Loading /> : null}
      {documents.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={documents.error} /> : null}
      {documents.data ? <DocumentList projectId={p.id} documents={documents.data} /> : null}
      <UploadForm project={p} />
      <DeleteProject project={p} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

### Task 6: A run: live progress, cancel and retry, and its overview

This task covers spec §8.2 (run status, live progress by stage, retry failed chunks) and §7.1 (progress over SSE with a polling fallback).

The run page has a header and four tabs:
- **Header:** the document, its languages, its state, and live progress. While the run is not finished, the browser subscribes to `GET /api/runs/{id}/events` with an `EventSource`.
- **Overview:** the run's summary, notices and statistics.
- **Guide** (Task 7–8), **Review** (Tasks 9–10) and **Export** (Task 11).

**Progress updates:**
- Each `progress` event replaces the cached progress.
- When the state changes, the run's detail is fetched again, so the summary and statistics appear when they exist.
- On a terminal state the client closes the stream. This is carry-over item m1: a browser would otherwise reconnect forever after the server ends the stream.
- Where `EventSource` is missing, the run is polled every 3 s instead.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `web/src/api/runs.ts`, `web/src/api/events.ts`
  - `web/src/pages/run/RunLayout.tsx`, `web/src/pages/run/RunProgressView.tsx`, `web/src/pages/run/RunActions.tsx`, `web/src/pages/run/RunOverview.tsx`
  - `web/src/test/fake-events.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/routes.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
- Test:
  - `web/src/api/events.test.ts`, `web/src/api/runs.test.ts`
  - `web/src/pages/run/RunLayout.test.tsx`, `web/src/pages/run/RunOverview.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–5): `client`, `unwrap`, `eventsUrl`, `isTerminal`, `StateBadge`, `languagePair`, `profileLabel`, `formatScore`, the fixtures.
- Produces:
  - **`web/src/api/runs.ts`**
    - `runKeys`:
      - `one(id) = ["runs", id]`
      - `guide(id) = ["runs", id, "guide"]`
      - `spans(id) = ["runs", id, "spans"]`
      - `span(id, spanId) = ["runs", id, "spans", spanId]`
      - Invalidating `["runs", id]` refreshes everything about a run.
    - `useRun(runId)` returns a `RunDetail` query.
    - `useRunAction(runId)` is a mutation `(kind: "cancel" | "retry" | "sweep") => ActionResult`.
    - `pollInterval(detail: RunDetail | undefined, live: boolean): number | false`.
  - **`web/src/api/events.ts`**
    - `type EventSourceLike`, `type EventSourceFactory`
    - `setEventSourceFactory(factory?: EventSourceFactory | null)`, test-only:
      - `undefined` → the browser's `EventSource` if present
      - `null` → none
    - `liveUpdatesAvailable(): boolean`
    - `useRunEvents(runId: string, active: boolean)`
  - **`useRunDetail(): RunDetail`** (`web/src/pages/run/RunLayout.tsx`): read inside a run tab through the outlet context.
  - **Routes:** `/runs/:runId` (`RunLayout`) with index `RunOverview`. Tasks 7–11 add `guide`, `review` and `export` as children.
  - **`FakeEventSource`** (`web/src/test/fake-events.ts`): `.url`, `.closed`, `emit(type, data)`, plus the static `FakeEventSource.instances`.
  - **Fixtures:** `runDetail: RunDetail`, a translating run.

`web/src/test/fake-events.ts`:

```ts
import type { EventSourceLike } from "../api/events";

/** A stand-in for the browser's EventSource (jsdom has none). */
export class FakeEventSource implements EventSourceLike {
  static instances: FakeEventSource[] = [];
  listeners = new Map<string, (event: MessageEvent<string>) => void>();
  closed = false;

  constructor(readonly url: string) {
    FakeEventSource.instances.push(this);
  }

  addEventListener(type: string, listener: (event: MessageEvent<string>) => void) {
    this.listeners.set(type, listener);
  }

  close() {
    this.closed = true;
  }

  emit(type: string, data: unknown) {
    this.listeners.get(type)?.({ data: JSON.stringify(data) } as MessageEvent<string>);
  }

  static last(): FakeEventSource {
    const last = FakeEventSource.instances.at(-1);
    if (!last) throw new Error("no EventSource was opened");
    return last;
  }
}
```
