```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { setEventSourceFactory } from "../../api/events";
import { FakeEventSource } from "../../test/fake-events";
import { runDetail } from "../../test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "../../test/render";
import { server } from "../../test/server";

beforeEach(() => {
  FakeEventSource.instances = [];
  setEventSourceFactory((url) => new FakeEventSource(url));
});
afterEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(undefined));

function serveRun(...details: object[]) {
  let calls = 0;
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1", () => {
      const body = details[Math.min(calls, details.length - 1)];
      calls += 1;
      return HttpResponse.json(body);
    }),
  );
  return () => calls;
}

it("shows the run, its state and its progress by stage", async () => {
  serveRun(runDetail);
  renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "histoire.txt" })).toHaveAttribute("dir", "auto");
  expect(screen.getByText("Translating")).toHaveClass("badge");
  expect(screen.getByText("fr → en")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByRole("progressbar", { name: "Translated" })).toHaveAttribute("value", "3");
  expect(screen.getByRole("progressbar", { name: "Reviewed" })).toHaveAttribute("value", "2");
  expect(screen.getByRole("progressbar", { name: "Scored" })).toHaveAttribute("max", "4");
  expect(screen.getByText("1 of 4 chunks scored")).toBeInTheDocument();
  const tabs = screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Run" });
  for (const name of ["Overview", "Guide", "Review", "Export"]) {
    expect(within(tabs).getByRole("link", { name })).toBeInTheDocument();
  }
  expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Back to the project" })).toHaveAttribute(
    "href",
    "/projects/p-1",
  );
});

it("follows the run live and stops listening when it ends", async () => {
  const done = {
    ...runDetail,
    run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "complete" },
    progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "complete" },
    report_available: true,
  };
  const calls = serveRun(runDetail, done);
  renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  await screen.findByText("1 of 4 chunks scored");
  const source = FakeEventSource.last();
  expect(source.url).toBe("/api/runs/r-1/events");
  source.emit("progress", {
    ...runDetail.progress,
    chunks_done: { translate: 4, reconcile: 4, score: 3 },
  });
  expect(await screen.findByText("3 of 4 chunks scored")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(calls()).toBe(1); // same state: no refetch
  source.emit("progress", { ...done.progress });
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("Complete")).toHaveClass("badge"));
  await waitFor(() => expect(calls()).toBe(2)); // the state changed: the detail is fetched again
  expect(source.closed).toBe(true);
});

it("does not subscribe to a finished run", async () => {
  serveRun({ ...runDetail, run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "complete" } });
  renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  await screen.findByText("Complete");
  expect(FakeEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(0);
});

it("notices a deleted run", async () => {
  let deleted = false;
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1", () =>
      deleted
        ? HttpResponse.json({ detail: "run r-1 not found" }, { status: 404 })
        : HttpResponse.json(runDetail),
    ),
  );
  renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  await screen.findByText("Translating");
  deleted = true;
  FakeEventSource.last().emit("deleted", {});
  expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("run r-1 not found");
  expect(FakeEventSource.last().closed).toBe(true);
});

it("explains a failed run", async () => {
  serveRun({
    ...runDetail,
    run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "failed", error: "scan.pdf needs OCR (needs_ocr): page(s) 2" },
    progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "failed", retryable: true },
  });
  renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  expect(await screen.findByText("scan.pdf needs OCR (needs_ocr): page(s) 2")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Cancel run" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("cancels a run and shows the answer", async () => {
  serveRun(runDetail);
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/cancel", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ message: "cancel requested", progress: runDetail.progress }),
    ),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Cancel run" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("cancel requested");
});

it("shows a refused action", async () => {
  serveRun({
    ...runDetail,
    run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "sweeping", finished_at: "2026-10-01T09:05:00Z" },
    progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "sweeping" },
  });
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/cancel", () =>
      HttpResponse.json(
        { detail: "run r-1 is reworking a finished translation and cannot be cancelled" },
        { status: 409 },
      ),
    ),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Cancel run" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("cannot be cancelled");
});

it("retries failed chunks", async () => {
  let posted = false;
  serveRun({
    ...runDetail,
    run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "complete_with_errors" },
    progress: {
      ...runDetail.progress,
      state: "complete_with_errors",
      chunks_with_errors: 1,
      retryable: true,
    },
  });
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/retry", () => {
      posted = true;
      return HttpResponse.json({ message: "retrying 1 chunk(s)", progress: runDetail.progress });
    }),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/runs/r-1");
  expect(await screen.findByText("1 chunk failed")).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("retrying 1 chunk(s)");
  expect(posted).toBe(true);
});
```

`web/src/api/events.ts`:

```ts
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { isTerminal } from "../lib/format";
import { eventsUrl } from "./client";
import { runKeys } from "./runs";
import type { RunDetail, RunProgress, RunState } from "./types";

export type EventSourceLike = {
  addEventListener(type: string, listener: (event: MessageEvent<string>) => void): void;
  close(): void;
};
export type EventSourceFactory = (url: string) => EventSourceLike;

let override: EventSourceFactory | null | undefined;

/** Tests replace the browser's EventSource; `undefined` restores it, `null` turns it off. */
export function setEventSourceFactory(factory?: EventSourceFactory | null): void {
  override = factory;
}

function factory(): EventSourceFactory | null {
  if (override !== undefined) return override;
  if (typeof EventSource === "undefined") return null;
  return (url) => new EventSource(url);
}

export function liveUpdatesAvailable(): boolean {
  return factory() !== null;
}

/** Spec §7.1: follow a run's progress over server-sent events while it runs. The stream ends
 * after a terminal state; the client closes it then, or a browser would reconnect forever. */
export function useRunEvents(runId: string, active: boolean): void {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  useEffect(() => {
    const open = factory();
    if (!active || open === null) return;
    const source = open(eventsUrl(runId));
    let last: RunState | null = null;
    const refetch = () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: runKeys.one(runId) });
    source.addEventListener("progress", (event) => {
      const progress = JSON.parse(event.data) as RunProgress;
      queryClient.setQueryData<RunDetail>(runKeys.one(runId), (old) =>
        old === undefined
          ? old
          : { ...old, progress, run: { ...old.run, state: progress.state, error: progress.error ?? null } },
      );
      if (last !== null && last !== progress.state) void refetch();
      last = progress.state;
      if (isTerminal(progress.state)) source.close();
    });
    source.addEventListener("deleted", () => {
      source.close();
      void refetch();
    });
    return () => source.close();
  }, [runId, active, queryClient]);
}
```

**Import cycle note.** `events.ts` imports `runKeys` from `runs.ts`, and `runs.ts` imports `liveUpdatesAvailable` from `events.ts`. ES modules handle the cycle, because neither file uses the other's export while it is being loaded, only inside functions. If ESLint or Vite warns about it, move `runKeys` into a new `web/src/api/keys.ts` and import it from there in both files.

`web/src/api/runs.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { isTerminal } from "../lib/format";
import { client, unwrap } from "./client";
import { liveUpdatesAvailable } from "./events";
import type { RunDetail } from "./types";

export const runKeys = {
  one: (runId: string) => ["runs", runId] as const,
  guide: (runId: string) => ["runs", runId, "guide"] as const,
  spans: (runId: string) => ["runs", runId, "spans"] as const,
  span: (runId: string, spanId: string) => ["runs", runId, "spans", spanId] as const,
};

export const POLL_MS = 3000;

/** Without server-sent events (spec §7.1's fallback), poll a run until it ends. */
export function pollInterval(detail: RunDetail | undefined, live: boolean): number | false {
  if (live || detail === undefined || isTerminal(detail.run.state)) return false;
  return POLL_MS;
}

export function useRun(runId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: runKeys.one(runId),
    queryFn: () => unwrap(client.GET("/api/runs/{run_id}", { params: { path: { run_id: runId } } })),
    refetchInterval: (query) => pollInterval(query.state.data, liveUpdatesAvailable()),
  });
}

export type RunActionKind = "cancel" | "retry" | "sweep";

export function useRunAction(runId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (kind: RunActionKind) => {
      const params = { params: { path: { run_id: runId } } };
      if (kind === "cancel") return unwrap(client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/cancel", params));
      if (kind === "retry") return unwrap(client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/retry", params));
      return unwrap(client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/sweep", params));
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: runKeys.one(runId) }),
  });
}
```

`web/src/pages/run/RunProgressView.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { RunDetail } from "../../api/types";

const STAGES = [
  ["translate", "Translated"],
  ["reconcile", "Reviewed"],
  ["score", "Scored"],
] as const;

export function RunProgressView({ detail }: { detail: RunDetail }) {
  const { progress, run } = detail;
  const total = progress.chunks_total;
  const failed = progress.chunks_with_errors;
  return (
    <div className="progress">
      {total > 0 ? (
        <>
          <div className="row">
            {STAGES.map(([key, label]) => (
              <label key={key} className="stage">
                {label}{" "}
                <progress aria-label={label} max={total} value={progress.chunks_done[key] ?? 0} />
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">
            {progress.chunks_done["score"] ?? 0} of {total} chunks scored
          </p>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {failed > 0 ? (
        <p className="error">{failed === 1 ? "1 chunk failed" : `${failed} chunks failed`}</p>
      ) : null}
      {run.error ? <p className="error">{run.error}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`<progress>` has the implicit role `progressbar`, and its `aria-label` names it. Its `value` and `max` are attributes, which the test reads.

`web/src/pages/run/RunActions.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useRunAction } from "../../api/runs";
import type { RunDetail } from "../../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import { isTerminal } from "../../lib/format";

export function RunActions({ detail }: { detail: RunDetail }) {
  const action = useRunAction(detail.run.id);
  const running = !isTerminal(detail.run.state);
  return (
    <div className="row actions">
      {running ? (
        <button type="button" disabled={action.isPending} onClick={() => action.mutate("cancel")}>
          Cancel run
        </button>
      ) : null}
      {detail.progress.retryable ? (
        <button type="button" disabled={action.isPending} onClick={() => action.mutate("retry")}>
          Retry
        </button>
      ) : null}
      {action.isSuccess ? (
        <span role="status" className="muted">
          {action.data.message}
        </span>
      ) : null}
      {action.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={action.error} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/pages/run/RunLayout.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link, NavLink, Outlet, useOutletContext, useParams } from "react-router";
import { useRunEvents } from "../../api/events";
import { useRun } from "../../api/runs";
import type { RunDetail } from "../../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../../components/Loading";
import { StateBadge } from "../../components/StateBadge";
import { isTerminal, languagePair, profileLabel } from "../../lib/format";
import { RunActions } from "./RunActions";
import { RunProgressView } from "./RunProgressView";

/** The run's detail, for the tabs rendered inside the run page. */
export function useRunDetail(): RunDetail {
  return useOutletContext<RunDetail>();
}

export function RunLayout() {
  const { runId = "" } = useParams();
  const run = useRun(runId);
  const detail = run.data;
  useRunEvents(runId, detail !== undefined && !isTerminal(detail.run.state));
  if (run.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={run.error} />;
  if (detail === undefined) return <Loading />;
  const r = detail.run;
  return (
    <section>
      <p>
        <Link to={`/projects/${r.project_id}`}>Back to the project</Link>
      </p>
      <h1 dir="auto">{r.filename}</h1>
      <p className="row">
        <StateBadge state={r.state} />
        <span>{languagePair(r.source_lang, r.target_lang)}</span>
        <span className="muted">{profileLabel(r.pipeline_profile)} profile</span>
      </p>
      <RunProgressView detail={detail} />
      <RunActions detail={detail} />
      <nav aria-label="Run" className="tabs">
        <NavLink to={`/runs/${runId}`} end>
          Overview
        </NavLink>
        <NavLink to={`/runs/${runId}/guide`}>Guide</NavLink>
        <NavLink to={`/runs/${runId}/review`}>Review</NavLink>
        <NavLink to={`/runs/${runId}/export`}>Export</NavLink>
      </nav>
      <Outlet context={detail} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

**Missing runs (404 while live).** The deleted-run test refetches after a `deleted` event. The refetch fails with a 404, so `run.isError` becomes true while the old data is still cached. Checking `isError` before `detail` makes the page show the error.

`web/src/pages/run/RunOverview.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from "react-router";
import type { RunDetail } from "../../api/types";
import { useRunDetail } from "./RunLayout";

const GENRES: Record<string, string> = {
  literary_prose: "Literary prose",
  poetry: "Poetry",
  technical: "Technical",
  academic: "Academic",
  legal: "Legal",
  news: "News",
  correspondence: "Correspondence",
  social_media: "Social media",
  transcript: "Transcript",
  other: "Other",
};

function Stats({ detail }: { detail: RunDetail }) {
  const { stats } = detail;
  if (stats.spans === 0) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <p>
        {stats.spans} passages · {stats.open_flags} open flags · {stats.edited_spans} edited
      </p>
      <ul className="inline">
        {Object.entries(stats.flags_by_code).map(([code, count]) => (
          <li key={code} className="badge">
            {code}: {count}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </>
  );
}

export function RunOverview() {
  const detail = useRunDetail();
  const runId = detail.run.id;
  return (
    <div>
      {detail.run.state === "awaiting_guide_approval" ? (
        <p className="notice">
          The guide is ready. <Link to={`/runs/${runId}/guide`}>Review and approve the guide</Link>{" "}
          to start translating.
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {detail.notices.map((notice) => (
        <p key={notice.code} className="banner-warn">
          {notice.explanation}
        </p>
      ))}
      <h2>Summary</h2>
      {detail.summary ? (
        <p dir="auto">{detail.summary}</p>
      ) : (
        <p className="muted">The summary appears once the guide is built.</p>
      )}
      {detail.language_tier ? <p>Language reliability tier {detail.language_tier}</p> : null}
      {detail.profile ? (
        <p className="muted">
          {GENRES[detail.profile.genre] ?? detail.profile.genre} · {detail.profile.tone_register} ·{" "}
          {detail.profile.word_count} words
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Stats detail={detail} />
      {detail.report_available ? (
        <p>
          <Link to={`/runs/${runId}/review`}>Review the translation</Link>
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

The test expects `"Literary prose · literary, past tense · 1840 words"` as one text node. JSX splits the text across expressions, but Testing Library's `getByText` matches an element's full text content, so the separate pieces inside one `<p>` match. Keep the pieces in a single `<p>`.

### Task 7: The guide editor and approving it at the checkpoint

Spec §8.3: the guide editor covers the summary, the glossary (edit and lock), the entities and the style guide, and Approve resumes the run.

The editor is one component used in two places: this task's checkpoint approval and Task 8's project guide. It keeps the edit local until it is submitted. While there are unsaved changes, it guards against losing them: a confirm dialog when navigating inside the app, and the browser's prompt when closing or reloading the tab (Review Focus 1).

**Checkpoint approval.** The approval sends the edited guide together with the version the edit started from, the `base_version`. Task 1 made the server refuse (409) when a newer project guide was saved in between. In that case the page offers to start again from the current project guide, and an approval based on that version passes.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `web/src/api/guide.ts`
  - `web/src/guide/GuideEditor.tsx`, `web/src/guide/GlossaryTable.tsx`, `web/src/guide/EntityTable.tsx`, `web/src/guide/GuideDisplay.tsx`
  - `web/src/components/UnsavedChanges.tsx`
  - `web/src/pages/run/RunGuide.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/routes.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
- Test: `web/src/guide/GuideEditor.test.tsx`, `web/src/pages/run/RunGuide.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–6):
  - API: `client`, `unwrap`, `ApiError`, `runKeys`, `projectKeys`
  - Run page: `useRunDetail`
  - Components: `ErrorMessage`, `Loading`
  - Test helpers: fixtures, `renderApp`, `server`, `setEventSourceFactory`
- Produces:
  - **`web/src/api/guide.ts`:**
    - `guideKeys`:
      - `project(id) = ["projects", id, "guide"]`
      - `versions(id) = ["projects", id, "guide", "versions"]`
      - `version(id, n) = ["projects", id, "guide", "versions", n]`
    - `useRunGuide(runId, enabled?)`
    - `fetchProjectGuide(projectId, version?): Promise<GuideView>`
    - `useApproveGuide(runId)`: a mutation of `{guide, base_version}` returning `ActionResult`
  - **`<GuideEditor initial submitLabel submit afterSubmit? pending error/>`:**
    - `submit(guide) => Promise<unknown>`, called with the edited guide.
    - On success the edit becomes the new baseline, the guard is released, and then `afterSubmit(guide)` runs.
    - The parent remounts the editor with `key` to load a different guide.
  - **`emptyEntity(): Entity`** and **`sameGuide(a, b): boolean`**, exported from `GuideEditor.tsx`.
  - **`<GuideDisplay guide version/>`:** a read-only view.
  - **`<UnsavedChanges when={() => boolean}/>`:**
    - Blocks in-app navigation with a dialog ("Leave without saving" / "Stay") while `when()` is true.
    - Warns on unload while `when()` is true.
  - **Routes:** `/runs/:runId/guide` → `RunGuide`.
  - **Fixtures:** `guideView: GuideView` (v1, an engine draft) and `guide: Guide`.

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from "react-router";
import type { Guide } from "../api/types";
import { guide } from "../test/fixtures";
import { GuideEditor, sameGuide } from "./GuideEditor";

function setup(submit = vi.fn(async () => undefined), error: unknown = null) {
  const afterSubmit = vi.fn();
  const router = createMemoryRouter(
    [
      {
        path: "/",
        element: (
          <GuideEditor
            initial={guide}
            submitLabel="Save"
            submit={submit}
            afterSubmit={afterSubmit}
            pending={false}
            error={error}
          />
        ),
      },
      { path: "/elsewhere", element: <p>Elsewhere</p> },
    ],
    { initialEntries: ["/"] },
  );
  render(<RouterProvider router={router} />);
  return { router, submit, afterSubmit, user: userEvent.setup() };
}

it("shows every part of the guide, with text in its own direction", () => {
  setup();
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Summary")).toHaveValue(guide.summary);
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Summary")).toHaveAttribute("dir", "auto");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide")).toHaveValue("Plain, past tense.");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Term 1")).toHaveValue("chat");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Rendering 1")).toHaveValue("cat");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Lock term 1")).not.toBeChecked();
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Name 1")).toHaveValue("Marie");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Lock name 1")).toBeChecked();
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Cultural references (one per line)")).toHaveValue(
    "la ville lumière",
  );
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" })).toBeDisabled(); // nothing changed
});

it("edits, adds, locks and removes entries, then submits the edit", async () => {
  const { user, submit, afterSubmit } = setup();
  await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText("Rendering 1"));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Rendering 1"), "the cat");
  await user.click(screen.getByLabelText("Lock term 1"));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add term" }));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Term 2"), "pluie");
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Rendering 2"), "rain");
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Notes 2"), "never 'shower'");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove name 1" }));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add name" }));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Name 1"), "Paul");
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Rendering of name 1"), "Paul");
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("Kind 1"), "place");
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Cultural references (one per line)"), "\n  \nle métro");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(afterSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledOnce());
  const sent = submit.mock.calls[0]![0] as Guide;
  expect(sent.glossary).toEqual([
    { term: "chat", rendering: "the cat", notes: "", locked: true },
    { term: "pluie", rendering: "rain", notes: "never 'shower'", locked: false },
  ]);
  expect(sent.entities).toEqual([
    {
      name: "Paul",
      kind: "place",
      rendering: "Paul",
      gender: null,
      forms_of_address: "",
      speech_style: "",
      locked: false,
    },
  ]);
  expect(sent.cultural_references).toEqual(["la ville lumière", "le métro"]);
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" })).toBeDisabled(); // the edit is the baseline
});

it("refuses incomplete entries", async () => {
  const { user } = setup();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add term" }));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Term 2"), "pluie");
  expect(screen.getByText("Every term needs a rendering.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" })).toBeDisabled();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove term 2" }));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add name" }));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Name 2"), "Paul");
  expect(screen.getByText("Every name needs a rendering.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText("Summary"));
  expect(screen.getByText("The summary must not be empty.")).toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("keeps the edit when submitting fails", async () => {
  const submit = vi.fn(async () => {
    throw new Error("refused");
  });
  const { user, afterSubmit } = setup(submit, new Error("the guide changed since you opened it"));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide"), " Short sentences.");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
  expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("the guide changed since you opened it");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide")).toHaveValue("Plain, past tense. Short sentences.");
  expect(afterSubmit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});

it("asks before leaving with unsaved changes", async () => {
  const { user, router } = setup();
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide"), "!");
  await router.navigate("/elsewhere");
  expect(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Unsaved changes" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Stay" }));
  expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide")).toHaveValue("Plain, past tense.!");
  void router.navigate("/elsewhere");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Leave without saving" }));
  expect(await screen.findByText("Elsewhere")).toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("warns the browser before unloading unsaved changes", async () => {
  const { user } = setup();
  const clean = new Event("beforeunload", { cancelable: true });
  window.dispatchEvent(clean);
  expect(clean.defaultPrevented).toBe(false);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide"), "!");
  const dirty = new Event("beforeunload", { cancelable: true });
  window.dispatchEvent(dirty);
  expect(dirty.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
});

it("compares guides by content", () => {
  expect(sameGuide(guide, structuredClone(guide))).toBe(true);
  expect(sameGuide(guide, { ...guide, summary: "x" })).toBe(false);
});
```

`web/src/pages/run/RunGuide.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { setEventSourceFactory } from "../../api/events";
import { guide, guideView, runDetail } from "../../test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "../../test/render";
import { server } from "../../test/server";

beforeEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(null));
afterEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(undefined));

const waiting = {
  ...runDetail,
  run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "awaiting_guide_approval" as const },
  progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "awaiting_guide_approval" as const },
};

function serveRun(detail: object = waiting) {
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1", () => HttpResponse.json(detail)),
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1/guide", () => HttpResponse.json(guideView)),
  );
}

it("approves the draft as edited, from its version, and goes back to the run", async () => {
  serveRun();
  let sent: unknown;
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/guide/approve", async ({ request }) => {
      sent = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json({
        message: "guide version 2 approved; translation queued",
        progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "translating" },
      }, { status: 202 });
    }),
  );
  const { user, router } = renderApp("/runs/r-1/guide");
  expect(await screen.findByText(/This is the draft guide for this document \(version 1\)/))
    .toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByLabelText("Lock term 1"));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve and translate" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/runs/r-1"));
  expect(sent).toEqual({
    guide: { ...guide, glossary: [{ ...guide.glossary[0], locked: true }] },
    base_version: 1,
  });
});

it("approves an unchanged draft", async () => {
  serveRun();
  let sent: unknown;
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/guide/approve", async ({ request }) => {
      sent = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json(
        { message: "guide version 2 approved; translation queued", progress: runDetail.progress },
        { status: 202 },
      );
    }),
  );
  const { user, router } = renderApp("/runs/r-1/guide");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Approve as it is" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/runs/r-1"));
  expect(sent).toEqual({ guide, base_version: 1 });
});

it("offers the current project guide when another save got there first", async () => {
  serveRun();
  const current = {
    guide: { ...guide, style_guide: "House style v2." },
    info: { ...guideView.info, version: 2, status: "approved" as const, author: "user" as const },
  };
  const bases: unknown[] = [];
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/guide/approve", async ({ request }) => {
      const body = (await request.json()) as { base_version: number };
      bases.push(body.base_version);
      return body.base_version === 1
        ? HttpResponse.json(
            {
              detail:
                "the project guide changed since this draft (now version 2); reload it and approve the current guide",
            },
            { status: 409 },
          )
        : HttpResponse.json({ message: "ok", progress: runDetail.progress }, { status: 202 });
    }),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", () => HttpResponse.json(current)),
  );
  const { user, router } = renderApp("/runs/r-1/guide");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Approve as it is" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("now version 2");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Start from the current project guide" }));
  expect(await screen.findByLabelText("Style guide")).toHaveValue("House style v2.");
  expect(screen.getByText(/You are editing the project guide \(version 2\)/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve as it is" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/runs/r-1"));
  expect(bases).toEqual([1, 2]);
});

it("shows the guide a run used, read-only, once it is translating", async () => {
  serveRun(runDetail);
  renderApp("/runs/r-1/guide");
  expect(await screen.findByText("Guide version 1 (draft, engine)")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByRole("cell", { name: "chat" })).toHaveAttribute("dir", "auto");
  expect(screen.getByText("Locked")).toBeInTheDocument(); // Marie
  expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /Approve/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("says when the guide is not built yet", async () => {
  serveRun({ ...runDetail, run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "profiling" } });
  renderApp("/runs/r-1/guide");
  expect(await screen.findByText("The guide is built after the document is profiled."))
    .toBeInTheDocument();
});
```

**About "Approve as it is":** the editor's submit button is disabled while nothing has changed, but approving an unchanged draft is the common case at the checkpoint. The checkpoint page therefore shows a second button, "Approve as it is", next to the editor. It submits the guide as the editor currently holds it, and it is enabled whenever the guide is valid. In the first test the user edits and then clicks "Approve and translate". The other tests use "Approve as it is".

`web/src/api/guide.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { client, unwrap } from "./client";
import { runKeys } from "./runs";
import type { Guide, GuideView } from "./types";

export const guideKeys = {
  project: (projectId: string) => ["projects", projectId, "guide"] as const,
  versions: (projectId: string) => ["projects", projectId, "guide", "versions"] as const,
  version: (projectId: string, version: number) =>
    ["projects", projectId, "guide", "versions", version] as const,
};

export function useRunGuide(runId: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: runKeys.guide(runId),
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(client.GET("/api/runs/{run_id}/guide", { params: { path: { run_id: runId } } })),
    enabled,
  });
}

/** The project guide (Task 1: approved, else the latest finished run's), or one version of it. */
export function fetchProjectGuide(projectId: string, version?: number): Promise<GuideView> {
  return unwrap(
    client.GET("/api/projects/{project_id}/guide", {
      params: { path: { project_id: projectId }, query: version === undefined ? {} : { version } },
    }),
  );
}

export function useApproveGuide(runId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: { guide: Guide; base_version: number }) =>
      unwrap(
        client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/guide/approve", {
          params: { path: { run_id: runId } },
          body,
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: runKeys.one(runId) }),
  });
}
```

`web/src/components/UnsavedChanges.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useBlocker } from "react-router";

/** While `when()` is true: ask before leaving inside the app, and let the browser warn on
 * reload or close. `when` is read at navigation time, so a just-saved form can leave at once. */
export function UnsavedChanges({ when }: { when: () => boolean }) {
  const blocker = useBlocker(() => when());
  useEffect(() => {
    const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      if (when()) event.preventDefault();
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", warn);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", warn);
  }, [when]);
  if (blocker.state !== "blocked") return null;
  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="Unsaved changes" className="dialog">
      <p>You have unsaved changes. Leave without saving them?</p>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => blocker.proceed()}>
        Leave without saving
      </button>{" "}
      <button type="button" onClick={() => blocker.reset()}>
        Stay
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { GlossaryEntry } from "../api/types";

type Props = { entries: GlossaryEntry[]; onChange: (entries: GlossaryEntry[]) => void };

export function GlossaryTable({ entries, onChange }: Props) {
  const update = (i: number, patch: Partial<GlossaryEntry>) =>
    onChange(entries.map((e, k) => (k === i ? { ...e, ...patch } : e)));
  return (
    <fieldset>
      <legend>Glossary</legend>
      <table aria-label="Glossary">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Term</th>
            <th>Rendering</th>
            <th>Notes</th>
            <th>Locked</th>
            <th />
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {entries.map((entry, i) => (
            <tr key={i}>
              <td>
                <input
                  aria-label={`Term ${i + 1}`}
                  dir="auto"
                  value={entry.term}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { term: e.target.value })}
                />
              </td>
              <td>
                <input
                  aria-label={`Rendering ${i + 1}`}
                  dir="auto"
                  value={entry.rendering}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { rendering: e.target.value })}
                />
              </td>
              <td>
                <input
                  aria-label={`Notes ${i + 1}`}
                  dir="auto"
                  value={entry.notes}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { notes: e.target.value })}
                />
              </td>
              <td>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  aria-label={`Lock term ${i + 1}`}
                  checked={entry.locked}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { locked: e.target.checked })}
                />
              </td>
              <td>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={`Remove term ${i + 1}`}
                  onClick={() => onChange(entries.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}
                >
                  Remove
                </button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onChange([...entries, { term: "", rendering: "", notes: "", locked: false }])}
      >
        Add term
      </button>
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

`web/src/guide/EntityTable.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Entity } from "../api/types";

export const ENTITY_KINDS = ["person", "place", "organisation", "work", "other"];

type Props = { entities: Entity[]; onChange: (entities: Entity[]) => void; onAdd: () => void };

export function EntityTable({ entities, onChange, onAdd }: Props) {
  const update = (i: number, patch: Partial<Entity>) =>
    onChange(entities.map((e, k) => (k === i ? { ...e, ...patch } : e)));
  const text = (i: number, field: "name" | "rendering" | "forms_of_address" | "speech_style", label: string) => (
    <input
      aria-label={label}
      dir="auto"
      value={entities[i]![field]}
      onChange={(e) => update(i, { [field]: e.target.value })}
    />
  );
  return (
    <fieldset>
      <legend>Names</legend>
      <table aria-label="Names">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Name</th>
            <th>Kind</th>
            <th>Rendering</th>
            <th>Gender</th>
            <th>Forms of address</th>
            <th>Speech style</th>
            <th>Locked</th>
            <th />
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {entities.map((entity, i) => (
            <tr key={i}>
              <td>{text(i, "name", `Name ${i + 1}`)}</td>
              <td>
                <select
                  aria-label={`Kind ${i + 1}`}
                  value={entity.kind}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { kind: e.target.value })}
                >
                  {[...new Set([...ENTITY_KINDS, entity.kind])].map((kind) => (
                    <option key={kind} value={kind}>
                      {kind}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </td>
              <td>{text(i, "rendering", `Rendering of name ${i + 1}`)}</td>
              <td>
                <input
                  aria-label={`Gender ${i + 1}`}
                  value={entity.gender ?? ""}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { gender: e.target.value || null })}
                  size={8}
                />
              </td>
              <td>{text(i, "forms_of_address", `Forms of address ${i + 1}`)}</td>
              <td>{text(i, "speech_style", `Speech style ${i + 1}`)}</td>
              <td>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  aria-label={`Lock name ${i + 1}`}
                  checked={entity.locked}
                  onChange={(e) => update(i, { locked: e.target.checked })}
                />
              </td>
              <td>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={`Remove name ${i + 1}`}
                  onClick={() => onChange(entities.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}
                >
                  Remove
                </button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <button type="button" onClick={onAdd}>
        Add name
      </button>
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

**Entity kinds.** The engine's `Entity.kind` is free text. `ENTITY_KINDS` lists the usual kinds, and the select also includes the entry's own kind, so an unusual kind from the engine is kept.

`web/src/guide/GuideEditor.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import type { Guide, Entity } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { UnsavedChanges } from "../components/UnsavedChanges";
import { EntityTable } from "./EntityTable";
import { GlossaryTable } from "./GlossaryTable";

export function emptyEntity(): Entity {
  return {
    name: "",
    kind: "person",
    rendering: "",
    gender: null,
    forms_of_address: "",
    speech_style: "",
    locked: false,
  };
}

export function sameGuide(a: Guide, b: Guide): boolean {
  return JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
}

function problems(guide: Guide): string[] {
  const found: string[] = [];
  if (!guide.summary.trim()) found.push("The summary must not be empty.");
  if (guide.glossary.some((g) => !g.term.trim() || !g.rendering.trim())) {
    found.push(
      guide.glossary.some((g) => !g.term.trim())
        ? "Every term needs a term and a rendering."
        : "Every term needs a rendering.",
    );
  }
  if (guide.entities.some((e) => !e.name.trim() || !e.rendering.trim())) {
    found.push(
      guide.entities.some((e) => !e.name.trim())
        ? "Every name needs a name and a rendering."
        : "Every name needs a rendering.",
    );
  }
  return found;
}

type Props = {
  initial: Guide;
  submitLabel: string;
  submit: (guide: Guide) => Promise<unknown>;
  afterSubmit?: (guide: Guide) => void;
  pending: boolean;
  error: unknown;
  /** Extra buttons next to submit; each gets the guide as edited (e.g. "Approve as it is"). */
  extraActions?: (guide: Guide, ready: boolean, send: (guide: Guide) => void) => React.ReactNode;
};

export function GuideEditor({
  initial,
  submitLabel,
  submit,
  afterSubmit,
  pending,
  error,
  extraActions,
}: Props) {
  const [baseline, setBaseline] = useState(initial);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(initial);
  const dirty = !sameGuide(draft, baseline);
  const dirtyRef = useRef(dirty);
  dirtyRef.current = dirty;
  const isDirty = useCallback(() => dirtyRef.current, []);
  const found = problems(draft);
  const ready = found.length === 0 && !pending;
  const set = (patch: Partial<Guide>) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, ...patch }));

  async function send(guide: Guide) {
    try {
      await submit(guide);
    } catch {
      return; // the parent shows `error`; the edit stays
    }
    dirtyRef.current = false; // release the guard before the parent navigates
    setBaseline(guide);
    setDraft(guide);
    afterSubmit?.(guide);
  }

  function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (ready && dirty) void send(draft);
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} aria-label="Guide" className="guide-editor">
      <UnsavedChanges when={isDirty} />
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="guide-summary">Summary</label>
        <textarea
          id="guide-summary"
          dir="auto"
          rows={3}
          value={draft.summary}
          onChange={(e) => set({ summary: e.target.value })}
        />
      </div>
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="guide-style">Style guide</label>
        <textarea
          id="guide-style"
          dir="auto"
          rows={3}
          value={draft.style_guide}
          onChange={(e) => set({ style_guide: e.target.value })}
        />
      </div>
      <GlossaryTable entries={draft.glossary} onChange={(glossary) => set({ glossary })} />
      <EntityTable
        entities={draft.entities}
        onChange={(entities) => set({ entities })}
        onAdd={() => set({ entities: [...draft.entities, emptyEntity()] })}
      />
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="guide-culture">Cultural references (one per line)</label>
        <textarea
          id="guide-culture"
          dir="auto"
          rows={3}
          value={draft.cultural_references.join("\n")}
          onChange={(e) =>
            set({
              cultural_references: e.target.value
                .split("\n")
                .map((line) => line.trim())
                .filter((line, i, all) => line !== "" || i === all.length - 1),
            })
          }
        />
      </div>
      {found.map((problem) => (
        <p key={problem} className="error">
          {problem}
        </p>
      ))}
      <div className="row">
        <button type="submit" disabled={!ready || !dirty}>
          {submitLabel}
        </button>
        {extraActions?.(draft, ready, (guide) => void send(guide))}
      </div>
      {error ? <ErrorMessage error={error} /> : null}
    </form>
  );
}
```

**Cultural references textarea.** The test types `"\n  \nle métro"` after the existing line. While typing, the textarea's value is rebuilt from the list on every keystroke:
- A blank line in the middle is dropped as soon as typing moves past it.
- The filter keeps a trailing empty line, so a new line can be started.
- The submitted list is therefore `["la ville lumière", "le métro"]`.

If this interaction misbehaves under user-event (for example the cursor jumps, or the trailing empty line is kept in the final list), store the textarea's raw text in its own state. Then derive the list on submit with `.split("\n").map(trim).filter(Boolean)`, and keep the test's expectation.

**The `extraActions` render prop.** It lets the checkpoint page add "Approve as it is" next to the submit button. That button sends the guide as currently edited through the same `send`, so the same guard release and baseline update apply. Import `type ReactNode` from "react" and use `ReactNode` instead of `React.ReactNode` if the namespace is not in scope.

```tsx
import type { Guide } from "../api/types";

export function GuideDisplay({ guide, label }: { guide: Guide; label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="guide-display">
      <p className="muted">{label}</p>
      <h3>Summary</h3>
      <p dir="auto">{guide.summary}</p>
      <h3>Style guide</h3>
      <p dir="auto">{guide.style_guide}</p>
      <h3>Glossary</h3>
      {guide.glossary.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="muted">No terms.</p>
      ) : (
        <table aria-label="Glossary">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Term</th>
              <th>Rendering</th>
              <th>Notes</th>
              <th />
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {guide.glossary.map((g, i) => (
              <tr key={i}>
                <td dir="auto">{g.term}</td>
                <td dir="auto">{g.rendering}</td>
                <td dir="auto">{g.notes}</td>
                <td>{g.locked ? "Locked" : ""}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      <h3>Names</h3>
      {guide.entities.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="muted">No names.</p>
      ) : (
        <table aria-label="Names">
          <tbody>
            {guide.entities.map((e, i) => (
              <tr key={i}>
                <td dir="auto">{e.name}</td>
                <td>{e.kind}</td>
                <td dir="auto">{e.rendering}</td>
                <td>{e.locked ? "Locked" : ""}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      {guide.cultural_references.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <h3>Cultural references</h3>
          <ul>
            {guide.cultural_references.map((c) => (
              <li key={c} dir="auto">
                {c}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export function versionLabel(info: { version: number; status: string; author: string }): string {
  return `Guide version ${info.version} (${info.status}, ${info.author})`;
}
```

`web/src/pages/run/RunGuide.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { ApiError } from "../../api/client";
import { fetchProjectGuide, useApproveGuide, useRunGuide } from "../../api/guide";
import type { GuideView, RunDetail } from "../../api/types";
import { GuideDisplay, versionLabel } from "../../guide/GuideDisplay";
import { GuideEditor } from "../../guide/GuideEditor";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../../components/Loading";
import { useRunDetail } from "./RunLayout";

const BEFORE_GUIDE = new Set(["created", "ingesting", "profiling", "building_guide"]);

function CheckpointGuide({ detail }: { detail: RunDetail }) {
  const runId = detail.run.id;
  const draft = useRunGuide(runId);
  const approve = useApproveGuide(runId);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [start, setStart] = useState<GuideView | null>(null);
  const loadProject = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => fetchProjectGuide(detail.run.project_id),
    onSuccess: (view) => {
      approve.reset();
      setStart(view);
    },
  });
  if (draft.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (draft.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={draft.error} />;
  const base = start ?? draft.data;
  const conflict = approve.error instanceof ApiError && approve.error.status === 409;
  const submit = (guide: GuideView["guide"]) =>
    approve.mutateAsync({ guide, base_version: base.info.version });
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="notice">
        {start
          ? `You are editing the project guide (version ${base.info.version}).`
          : `This is the draft guide for this document (version ${base.info.version}).`}{" "}
        Edit it if needed, then approve it to start translating.
      </p>
      <GuideEditor
        key={base.info.version}
        initial={base.guide}
        submitLabel="Approve and translate"
        submit={submit}
        afterSubmit={() => navigate(`/runs/${runId}`)}
        pending={approve.isPending}
        error={approve.error}
        extraActions={(guide, ready, send) => (
          <button type="button" disabled={!ready} onClick={() => send(guide)}>
            Approve as it is
          </button>
        )}
      />
      {conflict && start === null ? (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => loadProject.mutate()}>
          Start from the current project guide
        </button>
      ) : null}
      {loadProject.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={loadProject.error} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function UsedGuide({ runId }: { runId: string }) {
  const view = useRunGuide(runId);
  if (view.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (view.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={view.error} />;
  return <GuideDisplay guide={view.data.guide} label={versionLabel(view.data.info)} />;
}

export function RunGuide() {
  const detail = useRunDetail();
  if (BEFORE_GUIDE.has(detail.run.state)) {
    return <p className="muted">The guide is built after the document is profiled.</p>;
  }
  if (detail.run.state === "awaiting_guide_approval") return <CheckpointGuide detail={detail} />;
  return <UsedGuide runId={detail.run.id} />;
}
```

**Approving after a 409.** After "Start from the current project guide", the editor remounts (`key` changes) with the project guide, and its "Approve as it is" sends `base_version` 2. This matches the server's rule: a base equal to the project guide's version passes.

`web/src/routes.tsx`: add `{ path: "guide", element: <RunGuide /> }` to the `runs/:runId` children.

---

### Task 8: The project guide: edit, version history, and re-running the sweep after a locked change

Spec §8.3 says that editing a locked term after translation offers to re-run the sweep. The project guide is what new runs of the project build on (Task 1 defines it). It can be edited in two places:
- **Its own page**, `/projects/:id/guide`, which also lists every version and shows any old one.
- **The Guide tab of a finished run.** When a save changes a locked term or name there, the tab offers to re-run the consistency sweep on that run.

Saves send the version the edit started from. When someone else saved in between, the API refuses with 409. The page then offers to reload the current guide and say what reloading discards.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/api/guide.ts` (project guide, versions, save)
- Create: `web/src/guide/diff.ts`, `web/src/guide/ProjectGuideEditor.tsx`, `web/src/pages/ProjectGuidePage.tsx`
- Modify:
  - `web/src/pages/run/RunGuide.tsx` (finished runs)
  - `web/src/pages/ProjectPage.tsx` (link to the guide)
  - `web/src/routes.tsx`
- Test: `web/src/guide/diff.test.ts`, `web/src/pages/ProjectGuidePage.test.tsx`, `web/src/pages/run/RunGuideFinished.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–7):
  - API: `guideKeys`, `fetchProjectGuide`, `projectKeys`, `useRunAction`
  - Components: `GuideEditor`, `GuideDisplay`, `versionLabel`, `ApiError`
  - Fixtures: `guide`, `guideView`, `runDetail`, `project`
- Produces:
  - `useProjectGuide(projectId)`
  - `useGuideVersions(projectId)`
  - `useGuideVersion(projectId, version: number | null)` (disabled when `null`)
  - `useSaveProjectGuide(projectId)`: a mutation `{guide, base_version: number | null} → GuideView`
  - `lockedChanged(before: Guide, after: Guide): boolean`: true when the set of locked glossary pairs (term, rendering) or locked name pairs (name, rendering) differs. Terms and names are compared case-insensitively.
  - `<ProjectGuideEditor projectId onSaved?/>`, where `onSaved(before: Guide, after: Guide)` is called after a successful save.
  - Routes: `/projects/:projectId/guide` → `ProjectGuidePage`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`web/src/guide/diff.test.ts`:

```ts
import { guide } from "../test/fixtures";
import { lockedChanged } from "./diff";

const locked = { ...guide, glossary: [{ ...guide.glossary[0]!, locked: true }] };

it("notices changes to locked entries only", () => {
  expect(lockedChanged(guide, { ...guide, summary: "other" })).toBe(false);
  expect(lockedChanged(guide, locked)).toBe(true); // a term became locked
  expect(lockedChanged(locked, guide)).toBe(true); // ...or unlocked
  const renamed = { ...locked, glossary: [{ ...locked.glossary[0]!, rendering: "kitty" }] };
  expect(lockedChanged(locked, renamed)).toBe(true);
  const noted = { ...locked, glossary: [{ ...locked.glossary[0]!, notes: "a note" }] };
  expect(lockedChanged(locked, noted)).toBe(false);
  const recased = { ...locked, glossary: [{ ...locked.glossary[0]!, term: "Chat" }] };
  expect(lockedChanged(locked, recased)).toBe(false);
  const moved = { ...guide, entities: [{ ...guide.entities[0]!, rendering: "Mary" }] };
  expect(lockedChanged(guide, moved)).toBe(true); // Marie is locked
  const loose = { ...guide, entities: [{ ...guide.entities[0]!, locked: false }] };
  expect(lockedChanged(loose, { ...loose, entities: [{ ...loose.entities[0]!, rendering: "Mary" }] }))
    .toBe(false);
});
```

`web/src/pages/ProjectGuidePage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { guide, guideView, project } from "../test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "../test/render";
import { server } from "../test/server";

const v2 = {
  guide: { ...guide, style_guide: "House style." },
  info: { ...guideView.info, version: 2, status: "approved" as const, author: "user" as const, source_run_id: null },
};

function serve(current: object = guideView, versions: object[] = [guideView.info]) {
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1", () => HttpResponse.json(project)),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", ({ request }) => {
      const version = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("version");
      return HttpResponse.json(version === "1" ? guideView : current);
    }),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide/versions", () => HttpResponse.json(versions)),
  );
}

it("edits and saves the project guide from the version it showed", async () => {
  serve();
  let sent: unknown;
  server.use(
    http.put("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", async ({ request }) => {
      sent = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json(v2);
    }),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/projects/p-1/guide");
  expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Project guide" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Atlas" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/projects/p-1");
  expect(screen.getByText("Guide version 1 (draft, engine)")).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide"));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide"), "House style.");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save the project guide" }));
  expect(await screen.findByText("Guide version 2 (approved, user)")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(sent).toEqual({ guide: v2.guide, base_version: 1 });
});

it("offers to reload when someone else saved first", async () => {
  let saved = false;
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1", () => HttpResponse.json(project)),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", () => HttpResponse.json(saved ? v2 : guideView)),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide/versions", () => HttpResponse.json([guideView.info])),
    http.put("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", () => {
      saved = true; // someone else's save landed first
      return HttpResponse.json(
        {
          detail:
            "the guide changed since you opened it (now version 2, you edited version 1); reload it and apply your changes again",
        },
        { status: 409 },
      );
    }),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/projects/p-1/guide");
  await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText("Summary"), " More.");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save the project guide" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("reload it");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Reload the current guide (discards your edits)" }));
  expect(await screen.findByText("Guide version 2 (approved, user)")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Style guide")).toHaveValue("House style.");
  expect(screen.getByLabelText("Summary")).toHaveValue(guide.summary);
});

it("starts a guide for a project that has none", async () => {
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1", () => HttpResponse.json(project)),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", () =>
      HttpResponse.json(
        { detail: "project p-1 has no guide yet; it gets one when a run's guide is approved, a run finishes or someone saves one" },
        { status: 404 },
      ),
    ),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide/versions", () => HttpResponse.json([])),
  );
  let sent: unknown;
  server.use(
    http.put("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", async ({ request }) => {
      sent = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json({ ...v2, info: { ...v2.info, version: 1 } });
    }),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/projects/p-1/guide");
  expect(await screen.findByText(/has no guide yet/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText("No versions yet.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Summary"), "A city in the rain.");
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save the project guide" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(sent).toMatchObject({ base_version: null }));
  expect((sent as { guide: { summary: string } }).guide.summary).toBe("A city in the rain.");
});

it("lists versions and shows an old one", async () => {
  serve(v2, [v2.info, guideView.info]);
  const { user } = renderApp("/projects/p-1/guide");
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Versions" });
  const rows = within(table).getAllByRole("row");
  expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("2");
  expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("approved");
  expect(within(rows[2]!).getByRole("link", { name: "run" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/runs/r-1");
  await user.click(within(rows[2]!).getByRole("button", { name: "Show version 1" }));
  const shown = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Version 1" });
  expect(within(shown).getByText("Plain, past tense.")).toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("shows a project guide that cannot load", async () => {
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1", () => HttpResponse.json(project)),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ detail: "the database is unavailable: x" }, { status: 503 }),

    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide/versions", () =>

  renderApp("/projects/p-1/guide");
  expect((await screen.findAllByRole("alert"))[0]).toHaveTextContent("the database is unavailable");
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { setEventSourceFactory } from "../../api/events";
import { guide, guideView, runDetail } from "../../test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "../../test/render";
import { server } from "../../test/server";

beforeEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(null));
afterEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(undefined));

const complete = {
  ...runDetail,
  run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "complete" as const },
  progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "complete" as const },
  report_available: true,
};

function serve() {
  let sweeps = 0;
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1", () => HttpResponse.json(complete)),
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1/guide", () => HttpResponse.json(guideView)),
    http.get("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", () => HttpResponse.json(guideView)),
    http.put("*/api/projects/p-1/guide", async ({ request }) => {
      const body = (await request.json()) as { guide: typeof guide };
      return HttpResponse.json({
        guide: body.guide,
        info: { ...guideView.info, version: 2, status: "approved", author: "user" },
      });
    }),
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/sweep", () => {
      sweeps += 1;
      return HttpResponse.json(
        { message: "re-running the sweep with guide version 2", progress: complete.progress },
        { status: 202 },
      );
    }),
  );
  return () => sweeps;
}

it("offers to re-run the sweep after a locked term changes", async () => {

  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Re-run the sweep" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("re-running the sweep with guide version 2");
  expect(sweeps()).toBe(1);
});

it("does not offer the sweep for other changes", async () => {

  expect(await within(project).findByText("Guide version 2 (approved, user)")).toBeInTheDocument();

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Append to `web/src/api/guide.ts` (import `projectKeys` from `./projects`, and `useQueryClient` from TanStack):

```ts
export function useProjectGuide(projectId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: guideKeys.project(projectId),
    queryFn: () => fetchProjectGuide(projectId),
  });
}

export function useGuideVersions(projectId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: guideKeys.versions(projectId),
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(
        client.GET("/api/projects/{project_id}/guide/versions", {
          params: { path: { project_id: projectId } },
        }),
      ),
  });
}

export function useGuideVersion(projectId: string, version: number | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: guideKeys.version(projectId, version ?? 0),
    queryFn: () => fetchProjectGuide(projectId, version ?? undefined),
    enabled: version !== null,
  });
}

export function useSaveProjectGuide(projectId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: { guide: Guide; base_version: number | null }) =>
      unwrap(
        client.PUT("/api/projects/{project_id}/guide", {
          params: { path: { project_id: projectId } },
          body,
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess: (view) => {
      queryClient.setQueryData(guideKeys.project(projectId), view);
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: guideKeys.versions(projectId) });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.one(projectId), exact: true });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.all, exact: true });
    },
  });
}
```

**Two things to keep in mind:**
- `invalidateQueries` with the key `["projects", id]` would also catch `["projects", id, "guide"]` and refetch the guide just set. `exact: true` avoids that.
- With `retry: false` in tests, a 404 from `useProjectGuide` lands in `isError` at once. In production the 4xx is not retried either (Task 3's `retryOnce`).

`web/src/guide/diff.ts`:

```ts
import type { Guide } from "../api/types";

function lockedPairs(guide: Guide): string {
  const terms = guide.glossary
    .filter((g) => g.locked)
    .map((g) => `t:${g.term.toLocaleLowerCase()}=${g.rendering}`);
  const names = guide.entities
    .filter((e) => e.locked)
    .map((e) => `n:${e.name.toLocaleLowerCase()}=${e.rendering}`);
  return [...terms, ...names].sort().join("\n");
}

/** Spec §8.3: a change to a locked term or name is what a re-run of the sweep would enforce. */
export function lockedChanged(before: Guide, after: Guide): boolean {
  return lockedPairs(before) !== lockedPairs(after);
}
```

`web/src/guide/ProjectGuideEditor.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";
import { useProjectGuide, useSaveProjectGuide } from "../api/guide";
import type { Guide } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../components/Loading";
import { versionLabel } from "./GuideDisplay";
import { GuideEditor } from "./GuideEditor";

const EMPTY: Guide = {
  summary: "",
  style_guide: "",
  glossary: [],
  entities: [],
  cultural_references: [],
};

type Props = { projectId: string; onSaved?: (before: Guide, after: Guide) => void };

export function ProjectGuideEditor({ projectId, onSaved }: Props) {
  const current = useProjectGuide(projectId);
  const save = useSaveProjectGuide(projectId);
  if (current.isPending) return <Loading />;
  const missing = current.error instanceof ApiError && current.error.status === 404;
  if (current.isError && !missing) return <ErrorMessage error={current.error} />;
  const view = current.data;
  const base = view?.info.version ?? null;
  const initial = view?.guide ?? EMPTY;
  const conflict = save.error instanceof ApiError && save.error.status === 409;
  return (
    <div>
      {view ? (
        <p className="muted">{versionLabel(view.info)}</p>
      ) : (
        <ErrorMessage error={current.error} />
      )}
      <GuideEditor
        key={base ?? "none"}
        initial={initial}
        submitLabel="Save the project guide"
        submit={(guide) => save.mutateAsync({ guide, base_version: base })}
        afterSubmit={(guide) => onSaved?.(initial, guide)}
        pending={save.isPending}
        error={save.error}
      />
      {conflict ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            save.reset();
            void current.refetch();
          }}
        >
          Reload the current guide (discards your edits)
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Two notes on this component:**
- **Missing guide.** For a project with no guide, the 404's message ("…has no guide yet; it gets one when…") is shown with `role="alert"`. The test finds it by text. The empty editor below it lets a person start one.
- **Reloading after a 409.** The refetch brings the newer version; `key` changes, and the editor remounts with it. The guard is released implicitly, because the old editor unmounts; the in-app blocker only intercepts navigation, not a remount.

`web/src/pages/ProjectGuidePage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router";
import { useGuideVersion, useGuideVersions } from "../api/guide";
import { useProject } from "../api/projects";
import { GuideDisplay, versionLabel } from "../guide/GuideDisplay";
import { ProjectGuideEditor } from "../guide/ProjectGuideEditor";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../components/Loading";
import { formatDate } from "../lib/format";

function Versions({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  const versions = useGuideVersions(projectId);
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const old = useGuideVersion(projectId, shown);
  if (versions.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (versions.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={versions.error} />;
  if (versions.data.length === 0) return <p className="muted">No versions yet.</p>;
  return (
    <>
      <table aria-label="Versions">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Version</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>By</th>
            <th>Saved</th>
            <th>From</th>
            <th />
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {versions.data.map((v) => (
            <tr key={v.version}>
              <td>{v.version}</td>
              <td>{v.status}</td>
              <td>{v.author}</td>
              <td>{formatDate(v.created_at)}</td>
              <td>{v.source_run_id ? <Link to={`/runs/${v.source_run_id}`}>run</Link> : "—"}</td>
              <td>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setShown(v.version)}>
                  Show version {v.version}
                </button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {old.data ? (
        <section aria-label={`Version ${old.data.info.version}`}>
          <GuideDisplay guide={old.data.guide} label={versionLabel(old.data.info)} />
        </section>
      ) : null}
      {old.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={old.error} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}

export function ProjectGuidePage() {
  const { projectId = "" } = useParams();
  const project = useProject(projectId);
  return (
    <section>
      <p>{project.data ? <Link to={`/projects/${projectId}`} dir="auto">{project.data.name}</Link> : null}</p>
      <h1>Project guide</h1>
      <p className="muted">
        New runs of this project build on this guide. Saving makes a new approved version.
      </p>
      <ProjectGuideEditor projectId={projectId} />
      <h2>History</h2>
      <Versions projectId={projectId} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

**Version buttons.** The test clicks "Show version 1" by its accessible name. A button's name is its text, so the version number is part of the visible label. That is fine, and clearer for a screen reader than a bare "Show".

`web/src/pages/run/RunGuide.tsx`: keep `CheckpointGuide` and `UsedGuide`, and change the final branch of `RunGuide`. For `complete` and `complete_with_errors`, show the guide the run used and the project guide editor, and after a save that changes locked entries, the sweep offer:

```tsx
import { lockedChanged } from "../../guide/diff";
import { ProjectGuideEditor } from "../../guide/ProjectGuideEditor";
import { useRunAction } from "../../api/runs";

function FinishedRunGuide({ detail }: { detail: RunDetail }) {
  const [offer, setOffer] = useState(false);
  const sweep = useRunAction(detail.run.id);
  return (
    <div className="two-columns">
      <section aria-label="Used by this run">
        <h2>Used by this run</h2>
        <UsedGuide runId={detail.run.id} />
      </section>
      <section aria-label="Project guide">
        <h2>Project guide</h2>
        <ProjectGuideEditor
          projectId={detail.run.project_id}
          onSaved={(before, after) => setOffer(lockedChanged(before, after))}
        />
        {offer ? (
          <p className="notice">
            Locked terms or names changed. Re-run the consistency sweep on this translation so it
            follows them?{" "}
            <button type="button" disabled={sweep.isPending} onClick={() => sweep.mutate("sweep")}>
              Re-run the sweep
            </button>
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {sweep.isSuccess ? (
          <p role="status" className="muted">
            {sweep.data.message}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {sweep.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={sweep.error} /> : null}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export function RunGuide() {
  const detail = useRunDetail();
  const state = detail.run.state;
  if (BEFORE_GUIDE.has(state)) {
    return <p className="muted">The guide is built after the document is profiled.</p>;
  }
  if (state === "awaiting_guide_approval") return <CheckpointGuide detail={detail} />;
  if (state === "complete" || state === "complete_with_errors") {
    return <FinishedRunGuide detail={detail} />;
  }
  return <UsedGuide runId={detail.run.id} />;
}
```

### Task 9: Review: the passage table, filters, paging, the document header and keyboard navigation

This task covers spec §8.4, the review view: source | translation | meaning | style | flags.

**Header.** Above the table is a document header with the summary, a reliability banner and the run's statistics.

**Filters and paging:**
- The table can be filtered by flagged status, flag code and score range.
- Passages load one page at a time (50 per page). The API's numbering (`ReviewSpan.number`) stays the same whatever the filter.
- The filters and the page live in the URL, so a reload, the back button or a shared link keeps the reviewer's place (Review Focus 5).

**Keyboard navigation:**
- `n` and `p` move to the next and previous passage with an open flag, crossing page boundaries.
- `Enter` opens or closes the selected passage's details; `Esc` closes them.
- Keys typed into a field are left alone.
- The details panel itself arrives in Task 10; this task tracks which row is open.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `web/src/api/review.ts`
  - `web/src/review/filters.ts`, `web/src/review/ReviewPage.tsx`, `web/src/review/ReviewHeader.tsx`, `web/src/review/Filters.tsx`, `web/src/review/SpanRow.tsx`, `web/src/review/useFlagKeys.ts`
- Modify:
  - `web/src/api/runs.ts` (`runKeys.pages`)
  - `web/src/routes.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
- Test: `web/src/review/filters.test.ts`, `web/src/review/ReviewPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–6): `client`, `unwrap`, `runKeys`, `useRunDetail`, `formatScore`, the fixtures, `setEventSourceFactory`.
- Produces:
  - **Run keys:** `runKeys.pages(runId) = ["runs", runId, "spans", "page"]`.
  - **`web/src/review/filters.ts`:**
    - `type ReviewFilter = { flagged: boolean; code: FlagCode | null; minMeaning: number | null; maxMeaning: number | null; minStyle: number | null; maxStyle: number | null; page: number }`
    - `PAGE_SIZE = 50`, `FLAG_CODES: FlagCode[]`
    - `filterFromParams(params: URLSearchParams): ReviewFilter`
    - `paramsFromFilter(filter): URLSearchParams`, which omits defaults
    - `spanQuery(filter)`, the API query with `offset` and `limit`
  - **`web/src/api/review.ts`:** `useSpanPage(runId, filter)`, with the previous page kept while the next loads. Task 10 adds the detail and action hooks here.
  - **`<ReviewPage/>`** at `/runs/:runId/review`, which holds `selected: string | null` and `open: string | null` (span ids).
    - Task 10 replaces `renderPanel` (initially `() => null`).
  - **`<SpanRow span selected open onToggle panel/>`**.
  - **`useFlagKeys({ spans, selected, setSelected, open, setOpen, page, pages, goToPage })`**.
  - **Fixtures:** `reviewSpans: ReviewSpan[]` (p1 clean, p2 with two open flags, p3 with one dismissed and one open flag) and `spanPage(spans, total?, offset?): SpanPage`.

```ts
import { filterFromParams, paramsFromFilter, spanQuery } from "./filters";

it("reads filters from the URL and ignores nonsense", () => {
  const f = filterFromParams(
    new URLSearchParams("flagged=1&code=low_meaning&min_meaning=40&max_meaning=abc&max_style=150&page=3"),
  );
  expect(f).toEqual({
    flagged: true,
    code: "low_meaning",
    minMeaning: 40,
    maxMeaning: null,
    minStyle: null,
    maxStyle: 100,
    page: 3,
  });
  expect(filterFromParams(new URLSearchParams("code=bogus&page=-2&min_style=-5"))).toMatchObject({
    flagged: false,
    code: null,
    page: 1,
    minStyle: 0,
  });
});

it("writes only what differs from the defaults", () => {
  const f = filterFromParams(new URLSearchParams(""));
  expect(paramsFromFilter(f).toString()).toBe("");
  expect(paramsFromFilter({ ...f, flagged: true, minMeaning: 0, page: 2 }).toString()).toBe(
    "flagged=1&min_meaning=0&page=2",
  );
});

it("builds the API query", () => {
  const f = filterFromParams(new URLSearchParams("flagged=1&code=style_drift&max_style=60&page=2"));
  expect(spanQuery(f)).toEqual({
    flagged: true,
    code: "style_drift",
    max_style: 60,
    offset: 50,
    limit: 50,
  });
  expect(spanQuery(filterFromParams(new URLSearchParams()))).toEqual({ offset: 0, limit: 50 });
});
```

```tsx
  expect(rows()[1]).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");

  await user.keyboard("p"); // no earlier flag on this page: back to page 1, which has none
  await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.search).toBe(""));

it("toggles details with the mouse", async () => {
  serve();
  const { user } = renderApp("/runs/r-1/review");
  const button = await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Details of passage 1" });

```

**Moving to the previous flag across pages.** When there is no earlier flagged passage on the current page and the page is not the first, `p` goes to the previous page and selects its last flagged passage. On a page without flags, it selects the last passage. In the last test, page 1 has no flagged passage, so `p` lands on page 1 and selects its last row. The test asserts only the page change.
