`web/src/review/filters.ts`:

```ts
import type { FlagCode } from "../api/types";

export const PAGE_SIZE = 50;

export const FLAG_CODES: FlagCode[] = [
  "low_meaning",
  "style_drift",
  "agent_disagreement",
  "terminology_violation",
  "possible_omission",
  "cultural_or_wordplay",
  "engine_error",
  "low_reliability_language",
  "unscored",
];

export type ReviewFilter = {
  flagged: boolean;
  code: FlagCode | null;
  minMeaning: number | null;
  maxMeaning: number | null;
  minStyle: number | null;
  maxStyle: number | null;
  page: number;
};

function score(text: string | null): number | null {
  if (text === null || text.trim() === "") return null;
  const value = Number(text);
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return null;
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, value));
}

export function filterFromParams(params: URLSearchParams): ReviewFilter {
  const code = params.get("code");
  const page = Number.parseInt(params.get("page") ?? "1", 10);
  return {
    flagged: params.get("flagged") === "1",
    code: FLAG_CODES.includes(code as FlagCode) ? (code as FlagCode) : null,
    minMeaning: score(params.get("min_meaning")),
    maxMeaning: score(params.get("max_meaning")),
    minStyle: score(params.get("min_style")),
    maxStyle: score(params.get("max_style")),
    page: Number.isFinite(page) && page >= 1 ? page : 1,
  };
}

export function paramsFromFilter(f: ReviewFilter): URLSearchParams {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (f.flagged) params.set("flagged", "1");
  if (f.code) params.set("code", f.code);
  const scores: [string, number | null][] = [
    ["min_meaning", f.minMeaning],
    ["max_meaning", f.maxMeaning],
    ["min_style", f.minStyle],
    ["max_style", f.maxStyle],
  ];
  for (const [key, value] of scores) if (value !== null) params.set(key, String(value));
  if (f.page > 1) params.set("page", String(f.page));
  return params;
}

export type SpanQuery = {
  flagged?: boolean;
  code?: FlagCode;
  min_meaning?: number;
  max_meaning?: number;
  min_style?: number;
  max_style?: number;
  offset: number;
  limit: number;
};

export function spanQuery(f: ReviewFilter): SpanQuery {
  const query: SpanQuery = { offset: (f.page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, limit: PAGE_SIZE };
  if (f.flagged) query.flagged = true;
  if (f.code) query.code = f.code;
  if (f.minMeaning !== null) query.min_meaning = f.minMeaning;
  if (f.maxMeaning !== null) query.max_meaning = f.maxMeaning;
  if (f.minStyle !== null) query.min_style = f.minStyle;
  if (f.maxStyle !== null) query.max_style = f.maxStyle;
  return query;
}
```

**Key order.** The URL test expects `flagged`, `code`, then the scores, then `page`. `paramsFromFilter` writes them in that order. The API query test compares objects, so key order there does not matter.

`web/src/api/review.ts`:

```ts
import { keepPreviousData, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { spanQuery, type ReviewFilter } from "../review/filters";
import { client, unwrap } from "./client";
import { runKeys } from "./runs";

export function useSpanPage(runId: string, filter: ReviewFilter, enabled = true) {
  const query = spanQuery(filter);
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...runKeys.pages(runId), query],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(
        client.GET("/api/runs/{run_id}/spans", {
          params: { path: { run_id: runId }, query },
        }),
      ),
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    enabled,
  });
}
```

**Query types.** If `tsc` rejects `query` because the generated query type differs (for example `code?: FlagCode | null`), type `SpanQuery` as `paths["/api/runs/{run_id}/spans"]["get"]["parameters"]["query"]` from `../api/schema` and keep the function body.

`web/src/review/Filters.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import type { FlagCode } from "../api/types";
import { FLAG_CODES, type ReviewFilter } from "./filters";

type Props = { filter: ReviewFilter; onApply: (filter: ReviewFilter) => void };

function NumberField({ id, label, value, onChange }: { id: string; label: string; value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <span className="score-field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <input id={id} type="number" min={0} max={100} value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />
    </span>
  );
}

const text = (n: number | null) => (n === null ? "" : String(n));
const num = (s: string) => (s.trim() === "" ? null : Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(s))));

export function Filters({ filter, onApply }: Props) {
  const [flagged, setFlagged] = useState(filter.flagged);
  const [code, setCode] = useState<FlagCode | "">(filter.code ?? "");
  const [minMeaning, setMinMeaning] = useState(text(filter.minMeaning));
  const [maxMeaning, setMaxMeaning] = useState(text(filter.maxMeaning));
  const [minStyle, setMinStyle] = useState(text(filter.minStyle));
  const [maxStyle, setMaxStyle] = useState(text(filter.maxStyle));

  function apply(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    onApply({
      flagged,
      code: code || null,
      minMeaning: num(minMeaning),
      maxMeaning: num(maxMeaning),
      minStyle: num(minStyle),
      maxStyle: num(maxStyle),
      page: 1,
    });
  }

  function clear() {
    setFlagged(false);
    setCode("");
    setMinMeaning("");
    setMaxMeaning("");
    setMinStyle("");
    setMaxStyle("");
    onApply({ flagged: false, code: null, minMeaning: null, maxMeaning: null, minStyle: null, maxStyle: null, page: 1 });
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={apply} aria-label="Filters" className="filters">
      <label>
        <input type="checkbox" checked={flagged} onChange={(e) => setFlagged(e.target.checked)} /> Only
        passages with open flags
      </label>
      <span>
        <label htmlFor="filter-code">Flag</label>
        <select id="filter-code" value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value as FlagCode | "")}>
          <option value="">Any</option>
          {FLAG_CODES.map((c) => (
            <option key={c} value={c}>
              {c}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </span>
      <NumberField id="min-meaning" label="Meaning from" value={minMeaning} onChange={setMinMeaning} />
      <NumberField id="max-meaning" label="Meaning to" value={maxMeaning} onChange={setMaxMeaning} />
      <NumberField id="min-style" label="Style from" value={minStyle} onChange={setMinStyle} />
      <NumberField id="max-style" label="Style to" value={maxStyle} onChange={setMaxStyle} />
      <button type="submit">Apply filters</button>
      <button type="button" onClick={clear}>
        Clear filters
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

**The checkbox label.** The label text is "Only passages with open flags". The JSX line break inside it collapses to a single space, so `getByLabelText("Only passages with open flags")` matches.

`web/src/review/SpanRow.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import type { ReviewSpan } from "../api/types";
import { formatScore } from "../lib/format";

type Props = {
  span: ReviewSpan;
  selected: boolean;
  open: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
  onSelect: () => void;
  panel: ReactNode;
};

export function SpanRow({ span, selected, open, onToggle, onSelect, panel }: Props) {
  return (
    <>
      <tr aria-selected={selected} className={selected ? "selected" : undefined} onClick={onSelect}>
        <td>{span.number}</td>
        <td dir="auto" className="text">
          {span.source}
        </td>
        <td className="text">
          <span dir="auto">{span.text}</span>
          {span.edited ? <span className="badge edited">edited</span> : null}
        </td>
        <td>{formatScore(span.score.meaning)}</td>
        <td>{formatScore(span.score.style)}</td>
        <td>
          {span.flags.map((flag) => (
            <span key={flag.id} className={`badge flag-${flag.status}`} title={flag.explanation}>
              {flag.code}
            </span>
          ))}
        </td>
        <td>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-label={`Details of passage ${span.number}`}
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onToggle();
            }}
          >
            {open ? "Hide" : "Details"}
          </button>
        </td>
      </tr>
      {open && panel ? (
        <tr className="panel-row">
          <td colSpan={7}>{panel}</td>
        </tr>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

**Row counts with the panel open.** The panel row only exists while the panel is open and `panel` is not null. Task 9's `renderPanel` returns `null`, so the row counts in the tests stay 1 + passages. In Task 10, rows counted in tests are counted with no panel open.

`web/src/review/useFlagKeys.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { ReviewSpan } from "../api/types";

const hasOpenFlag = (span: ReviewSpan) => span.flags.some((f) => f.status === "open");

function typing(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  return target.isContentEditable || ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(target.tagName);
}

export type Pending = "first-flag" | "last-flag" | null;

type Options = {
  spans: ReviewSpan[];
  selected: string | null;
  setSelected: (id: string | null) => void;
  open: string | null;
  setOpen: (id: string | null) => void;
  page: number;
  pages: number;
  goToPage: (page: number, then: Pending) => void;
};

/** Spec §8.4: keyboard navigation between flags. n/p: next/previous passage with an open flag
 * (across pages); Enter: open or close the selected passage; Escape: close it. */
export function useFlagKeys(options: Options): void {
  const ref = useRef(options);
  ref.current = options;
  useEffect(() => {
    function onKey(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || typing(event.target)) return;
      const { spans, selected, setSelected, open, setOpen, page, pages, goToPage } = ref.current;
      const index = spans.findIndex((s) => s.id === selected);
      if (event.key === "n" || event.key === "p") {
        const forward = event.key === "n";
        const order = forward ? spans.slice(index + 1) : spans.slice(0, Math.max(index, 0)).reverse();
        const next = order.find(hasOpenFlag);
        if (next) setSelected(next.id);
        else if (forward && page < pages) goToPage(page + 1, "first-flag");
        else if (!forward && page > 1) goToPage(page - 1, "last-flag");
        event.preventDefault();
      } else if (event.key === "Enter" && selected !== null) {
        setOpen(open === selected ? null : selected);
        event.preventDefault();
      } else if (event.key === "Escape" && open !== null) {
        setOpen(null);
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, []);
}
```

**Selection when going back.** When `p` is pressed with nothing selected (`index` is -1), there is nothing before the selection, so the order is empty. The hook then goes to the previous page when there is one, and otherwise does nothing.

`web/src/review/ReviewPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router";
import { useSpanPage } from "../api/review";
import type { ReviewSpan } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../components/Loading";
import { useRunDetail } from "../pages/run/RunLayout";
import { Filters } from "./Filters";
import { filterFromParams, PAGE_SIZE, paramsFromFilter, type ReviewFilter } from "./filters";
import { ReviewHeader } from "./ReviewHeader";
import { SpanRow } from "./SpanRow";
import { useFlagKeys, type Pending } from "./useFlagKeys";

/** Task 10 replaces this with the passage panel. */
export const renderPanel = (_runId: string, _span: ReviewSpan): ReactNode => null;

export function ReviewPage() {
  const detail = useRunDetail();
  const runId = detail.run.id;
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const filter = filterFromParams(params);
  const page = useSpanPage(runId, filter, detail.report_available);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<Pending>(null);
  const spans = page.data?.spans ?? [];
  const total = page.data?.total ?? 0;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE));

  const apply = (next: ReviewFilter) => setParams(paramsFromFilter(next));
  const goToPage = (n: number, then: Pending) => {
    setPending(then);
    apply({ ...filter, page: n });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pending === null || page.isPlaceholderData || page.data === undefined) return;
    const flagged = page.data.spans.filter((s) => s.flags.some((f) => f.status === "open"));
    const pool = flagged.length > 0 ? flagged : page.data.spans;
    const target = pending === "first-flag" ? pool[0] : pool.at(-1);
    setSelected(target?.id ?? null);
    setPending(null);
  }, [pending, page.data, page.isPlaceholderData]);

  useFlagKeys({ spans, selected, setSelected, open, setOpen, page: filter.page, pages, goToPage });

  if (!detail.report_available) {
    return <p className="muted">The review opens when the translation is complete.</p>;
  }
  const first = (filter.page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1;
  return (
    <div>
      <ReviewHeader detail={detail} />
      <Filters key={params.toString()} filter={filter} onApply={apply} />
      <p className="muted keys">Keys: n next flag · p previous flag · Enter open or close · Esc close</p>
      {page.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={page.error} /> : null}
      {page.isPending ? <Loading /> : null}
      {page.data && spans.length === 0 ? <p className="muted">No passage matches these filters.</p> : null}
      {spans.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <table aria-label="Passages" className="passages">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>#</th>
                <th>Source</th>
                <th>Translation</th>
                <th>Meaning</th>
                <th>Style</th>
                <th>Flags</th>
                <th />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {spans.map((span) => (
                <SpanRow
                  key={span.id}
                  span={span}
                  selected={selected === span.id}
                  open={open === span.id}
                  onSelect={() => setSelected(span.id)}
                  onToggle={() => setOpen(open === span.id ? null : span.id)}
                  panel={open === span.id ? renderPanel(runId, span) : null}
                />
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          <div className="row pager">
            <button type="button" aria-label="Previous page" disabled={filter.page <= 1} onClick={() => goToPage(filter.page - 1, null)}>
              ←
            </button>
            <span>
              Passages {first}–{first + spans.length - 1} of {total}
            </span>
            <button type="button" aria-label="Next page" disabled={filter.page >= pages} onClick={() => goToPage(filter.page + 1, null)}>
              →
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Notes on this page:**
- **Panel row and table rows.** Task 9 never renders a panel row because `renderPanel` returns `null` (see `SpanRow`). The keyboard test counts `tbody` rows through `getAllByRole("row").slice(1)` with no panel open.
- **"Toggles details with the mouse".** Opening sets `open`. The panel row then appears in Task 10, not here.
- **The filters form.** `Filters` takes its starting values from the URL. Its `key` changes when the URL changes, so a reload or back button refreshes the form.
- **Rules of hooks.** `useSpanPage(..., enabled = detail.report_available)` and the hooks are called before the early return.
- **The pager text.** "Passages 1–50 of 51" uses an en dash. The test strings use the same character.

`web/src/routes.tsx`: add `{ path: "review", element: <ReviewPage /> }` to the run's children.

### Task 10: Review: the passage panel and the review actions

Spec §8.4 calls for an expandable panel per passage and a set of review actions.

**The panel shows:**
- the candidates with their role and model
- the reviewer's rationale
- the signal breakdown
- the judge's error spans highlighted inline
- the revision history.

**The actions are:**
- accept
- use a candidate
- edit
- accept, dismiss (with a reason) or reopen a flag
- add a term or a name to the guide.

Each action posts to the M4a API. The API records it in the feedback log, then answers with the passage's new detail, which replaces the cached one. The page of passages and the run's statistics are fetched again.

**Refused actions (Review Focus 3).** An action can be refused, for example with a 409 while the run is sweeping. When that happens, the message is shown and nothing the reviewer typed is lost.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/api/review.ts`, `web/src/review/ReviewPage.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
- Create:
  - `web/src/review/highlight.ts`
  - `web/src/review/SpanPanel.tsx`, `web/src/review/EditTranslation.tsx`, `web/src/review/FlagList.tsx`, `web/src/review/Candidates.tsx`, `web/src/review/Findings.tsx`, `web/src/review/Signals.tsx`, `web/src/review/Revisions.tsx`, `web/src/review/AddToGuide.tsx`
- Test: `web/src/review/highlight.test.ts`, `web/src/review/SpanPanel.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–9):
  - Keys: `runKeys.span`, `runKeys.pages`, `runKeys.one`, `guideKeys.project`, `guideKeys.versions`, `projectKeys.one`
  - Helpers: `emptyEntity`, `ENTITY_KINDS`, `formatDate`, `formatScore`
  - Review page: `ReviewPage`'s `open` state and `SpanRow`'s `panel`
  - Fixtures: `reviewSpans`, `spanPage`, `runDetail`
- Produces:
  - **Hooks in `web/src/api/review.ts`:**
    - `useSpanDetail(runId, spanId)`
    - `useEditSpan(runId)`: `{spanId, text, reason}`
    - `useChooseCandidate(runId)`: `{spanId, translator}`
    - `useAcceptSpan(runId)`: `spanId`
    - `useFlagStatus(runId)`: `{flagId, status, reason}`
    - `useAddToGuide(runId, projectId)`: `{spanId, addition}`
    - The span actions resolve to the new `SpanDetail`. `useAddToGuide` resolves to the new `GuideView`.
  - **`highlight(text, excerpts): {text: string; mark: boolean}[]`.**
  - **`<SpanPanel runId projectId span/>`.**
  - **Fixture `spanDetail: SpanDetail`** (passage 2).

import { highlight } from "./highlight";

it("marks every occurrence of each excerpt", () => {
  expect(highlight("The cat sleeps. The cat sleeps.", ["sleeps"])).toEqual([
    { text: "The cat ", mark: false },
    { text: "sleeps", mark: true },
    { text: ". The cat ", mark: false },

    { text: ".", mark: false },
  ]);
});

it("merges overlapping excerpts and ignores empty ones", () => {
  expect(highlight("abcdef", ["bcd", "cde", ""])).toEqual([
    { text: "a", mark: false },
    { text: "bcde", mark: true },
    { text: "f", mark: false },
  ]);
  expect(highlight("abc", [])).toEqual([{ text: "abc", mark: false }]);
  expect(highlight("", ["x"])).toEqual([]);
});

`web/src/review/SpanPanel.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { setEventSourceFactory } from "../api/events";
import { reviewSpans, runDetail, spanDetail, spanPage } from "../test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "../test/render";
import { server } from "../test/server";

beforeEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(null));
afterEach(() => setEventSourceFactory(undefined));

const complete = {
  ...runDetail,
  run: { ...runDetail.run, state: "complete" as const },
  progress: { ...runDetail.progress, state: "complete" as const },
  report_available: true,
  stats: { spans: 3, open_flags: 3, flags_by_code: {}, edited_spans: 0 },
};

function serve() {
  const counts = { pages: 0, run: 0 };
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1", () => {
      counts.run += 1;
      return HttpResponse.json(complete);
    }),
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1/spans", () => {
      counts.pages += 1;
      return HttpResponse.json(spanPage(reviewSpans));
    }),
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2", () => HttpResponse.json(spanDetail)),
  );
  return counts;
}

async function openPassage2() {
  const rendered = renderApp("/runs/r-1/review");
  await rendered.user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Details of passage 2" }));
  const panel = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Passage 2" });
  return { ...rendered, panel };
}

it("explains the passage", async () => {
  serve();
  const { panel } = await openPassage2();
  const candidates = within(panel).getByRole("list", { name: "Candidates" });
  const items = within(candidates).getAllByRole("listitem");
  expect(items).toHaveLength(2); // the literal pieces are one candidate
  expect(items[0]).toHaveTextContent("literal");
  expect(items[0]).toHaveTextContent("m1");
  expect(items[0]).toHaveTextContent("(chosen)");
  expect(within(items[0]!).getByText("The cat sleeps. It rains.")).toHaveAttribute("dir", "auto");
  expect(within(items[1]!).getByText("The cat is asleep. It rains.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(panel).getByText("Kept the literal tense.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(panel).getByText("tense of 'dort'")).toBeInTheDocument();
  const marked = within(panel).getByLabelText("Translation with the judge's findings");
  expect(within(marked).getByText("sleeps").tagName).toBe("MARK");
  expect(within(panel).getByText(/'Dort' here means 'is asleep'\./)).toBeInTheDocument();
  const signals = within(panel).getByRole("table", { name: "Signals" });
  expect(within(signals).getByRole("row", { name: /qe 0\.55/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(signals).getByRole("row", { name: /roundtrip —/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
  const history = within(panel).getByRole("list", { name: "History" });
  expect(history).toHaveTextContent("engine");
  expect(history).toHaveTextContent("reconciled");
  expect(within(panel).getByText("Le chat dort. Il pleut.", { selector: ".back-translation" }))
    .toHaveAttribute("dir", "auto");
});

it("edits the translation with a reason", async () => {
  const counts = serve();
  let sent: unknown;
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2/edit", async ({ request }) => {
      sent = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json({
        ...spanDetail,
        text: "The cat is asleep. It is raining.",
        edited: true,
        revisions: [
          ...spanDetail.revisions,
          { source: "user", before: spanDetail.text, after: "The cat is asleep. It is raining.", reason: "tense", created_at: "2026-10-02T10:00:00Z" },
        ],
      });
    }),
  );
  const { user, panel } = await openPassage2();
  const pagesBefore = counts.pages;
  const box = within(panel).getByLabelText("Translation of passage 2");
  expect(box).toHaveAttribute("dir", "auto");
  await user.clear(box);
  await user.type(box, "The cat is asleep. It is raining.");
  await user.type(within(panel).getByLabelText("Reason for the edit (optional)"), "tense");
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Save edit" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(within(panel).getByRole("list", { name: "History" })).toHaveTextContent("user"));
  expect(sent).toEqual({ text: "The cat is asleep. It is raining.", reason: "tense" });
  expect(within(panel).getByLabelText("Translation of passage 2")).toHaveValue("The cat is asleep. It is raining.");
  expect(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Save edit" })).toBeDisabled();
  await waitFor(() => expect(counts.pages).toBeGreaterThan(pagesBefore));
});

it("keeps the reviewer's text when an edit is refused", async () => {
  serve();
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2/edit", () =>
      HttpResponse.json(
        { detail: "run r-1 is re-running its consistency sweep; try again when it finishes" },
        { status: 409 },
      ),
    ),
  );
  const { user, panel } = await openPassage2();
  const box = within(panel).getByLabelText("Translation of passage 2");
  await user.type(box, " Indeed.");
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Save edit" }));
  expect(await within(panel).findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("try again when it finishes");
  expect(box).toHaveValue("The cat sleeps. It rains. Indeed.");
});

it("uses a candidate and accepts the passage", async () => {
  serve();
  const calls: string[] = [];
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2/use-candidate", async ({ request }) => {
      calls.push(`use:${((await request.json()) as { translator: string }).translator}`);
      return HttpResponse.json({ ...spanDetail, text: "The cat is asleep. It rains.", chosen_from: "contextual" });
    }),
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2/accept", () => {
      calls.push("accept");
      return HttpResponse.json({
        ...spanDetail,
        flags: spanDetail.flags.map((f) => ({ ...f, status: "accepted" })),
      });
    }),
  );
  const { user, panel } = await openPassage2();
  expect(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Use the literal translation" })).toBeDisabled();
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Use the contextual translation" }));
  await waitFor(() =>
    expect(within(panel).getByLabelText("Translation of passage 2")).toHaveValue("The cat is asleep. It rains."),
  );
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Accept passage" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(within(panel).getAllByText(/\(accepted\)/)).toHaveLength(2));
  expect(calls).toEqual(["use:contextual", "accept"]);
});

it("accepts, dismisses with a reason, and reopens flags", async () => {
  serve();
  const sent: unknown[] = [];
  let flags = spanDetail.flags; // the server's state: each answer builds on the last
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/flags/:flagId", async ({ request, params }) => {
      const body = (await request.json()) as { status: "open" | "accepted" | "dismissed"; reason?: string | null };
      sent.push({ flag: params["flagId"], ...body });
      flags = flags.map((f) =>
        String(f.id) === params["flagId"]
          ? { ...f, status: body.status, dismiss_reason: body.status === "dismissed" ? (body.reason ?? null) : null }
          : f,
      );
      return HttpResponse.json({ ...spanDetail, flags });
    }),
  );
  const { user, panel } = await openPassage2();
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss the low_meaning flag" }));
  const dismiss = within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss" });
  expect(dismiss).toBeDisabled();
  await user.type(within(panel).getByLabelText("Why dismiss the low_meaning flag?"), "intended tense");
  await user.click(dismiss);
  expect(await within(panel).findByText(/dismissed: intended tense/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Accept the agent_disagreement flag" }));
  await user.click(await within(panel).findByRole("button", { name: "Reopen the low_meaning flag" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(sent).toHaveLength(3));
  expect(sent).toEqual([
    { flag: "11", status: "dismissed", reason: "intended tense" },
    { flag: "12", status: "accepted", reason: null },
    { flag: "11", status: "open", reason: null },
  ]);
});

it("cancels a dismissal", async () => {
  serve();
  const { user, panel } = await openPassage2();
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss the low_meaning flag" }));
  await user.click(within(panel).getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel" }));
  expect(within(panel).queryByLabelText("Why dismiss the low_meaning flag?")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("adds a term and a name to the project guide", async () => {
  serve();
  const sent: unknown[] = [];
  server.use(
    http.post("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2/guide", async ({ request }) => {
      sent.push(await request.json());
      return HttpResponse.json({
        guide: { summary: "s", style_guide: "g", glossary: [], entities: [], cultural_references: [] },
        info: { version: 2 + sent.length, status: "approved", author: "user", created_at: "2026-10-02T10:00:00Z", source_run_id: "r-1" },
      });
    }),
  );
  const { user, panel } = await openPassage2();
  const form = within(panel).getByRole("group", { name: "Add to the project guide" });
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Source term"), "chat");
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Rendering"), "cat");
  await user.click(within(form).getByLabelText("Locked"));
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Add to the project guide" }));
  expect(await within(form).findByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("Added to the project guide (version 3).");
  await user.click(within(form).getByLabelText("Name"));
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Name in the source"), "Marie");
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Rendering"), "Mary");
  await user.selectOptions(within(form).getByLabelText("Kind"), "person");
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Add to the project guide" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(sent).toHaveLength(2));
  expect(sent[0]).toEqual({ glossary: { term: "chat", rendering: "cat", notes: "", locked: true } });
  expect(sent[1]).toEqual({
    entity: {
      name: "Marie",
      kind: "person",
      rendering: "Mary",
      gender: null,
      forms_of_address: "",
      speech_style: "",
      locked: false,
    },
  });
});

it("shows a passage that cannot load", async () => {
  serve();
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/runs/r-1/spans/s-2", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ detail: "span s-2 not found in run r-1" }, { status: 404 }),
    ),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/runs/r-1/review");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Details of passage 2" }));
  expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("span s-2 not found");
});
```

**Notes on these tests:**
- **`qe` signal value.** The fixture's `score()` helper gives `qe` = meaning / 100, so passage 2 has `qe` = 0.55. The `Signals` table shows two decimals.
- **Radio labels.** "Name" is the radio label in the guide form. The test clicks it by label, then fills "Name in the source". If `getByLabelText("Name")` is ambiguous because "Name in the source" also matches, use exact matching. Testing Library matches exact text by default, so "Name" does not match "Name in the source".

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

Append to `web/src/api/review.ts` (update imports: `useMutation`, `useQueryClient`; `guideKeys` from `./guide`; `projectKeys` from `./projects`; types `Entity`, `GlossaryEntry`, `SpanDetail`):

```ts
export function useSpanDetail(runId: string, spanId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: runKeys.span(runId, spanId),
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(
        client.GET("/api/runs/{run_id}/spans/{span_id}", {
          params: { path: { run_id: runId, span_id: spanId } },
        }),
      ),
  });
}

/** After a review action: the answer is the passage's new detail; the page of passages and the
 * run's statistics change with it. */
function useSpanChanged(runId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return (detail: SpanDetail) => {
    queryClient.setQueryData(runKeys.span(runId, detail.id), detail);
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: runKeys.pages(runId) });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: runKeys.one(runId), exact: true });
  };
}

const spanPath = (runId: string, spanId: string) => ({
  params: { path: { run_id: runId, span_id: spanId } },
});

export function useEditSpan(runId: string) {
  const onSuccess = useSpanChanged(runId);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ spanId, text, reason }: { spanId: string; text: string; reason: string | null }) =>
      unwrap(
        client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/spans/{span_id}/edit", {
          ...spanPath(runId, spanId),
          body: { text, reason },
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess,
  });
}

export function useChooseCandidate(runId: string) {
  const onSuccess = useSpanChanged(runId);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ spanId, translator }: { spanId: string; translator: string }) =>
      unwrap(
        client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/spans/{span_id}/use-candidate", {
          ...spanPath(runId, spanId),
          body: { translator },
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess,
  });
}

export function useAcceptSpan(runId: string) {
  const onSuccess = useSpanChanged(runId);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (spanId: string) =>
      unwrap(client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/spans/{span_id}/accept", spanPath(runId, spanId))),
    onSuccess,
  });
}

export type FlagChange = { flagId: number; status: "accepted" | "dismissed" | "open"; reason: string | null };

export function useFlagStatus(runId: string) {
  const onSuccess = useSpanChanged(runId);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ flagId, status, reason }: FlagChange) =>
      unwrap(
        client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/flags/{flag_id}", {
          params: { path: { run_id: runId, flag_id: flagId } },
          body: { status, reason },
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess,
  });
}

export type GuideAddition = { glossary: GlossaryEntry } | { entity: Entity };

export function useAddToGuide(runId: string, projectId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ spanId, addition }: { spanId: string; addition: GuideAddition }) =>
      unwrap(
        client.POST("/api/runs/{run_id}/spans/{span_id}/guide", {
          ...spanPath(runId, spanId),
          body: addition,
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess: (view) => {
      queryClient.setQueryData(guideKeys.project(projectId), view);
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: guideKeys.versions(projectId) });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKeys.one(projectId), exact: true });
    },
  });
}
```

**Typing the bodies:**
- `useEditSpan` sends `reason: null` when no reason is given. The test expects `{ text, reason: "tense" }` when one is.
- If the generated `EditSpan`/`FlagUpdate` types make `reason` `string | null | undefined`, the calls type-check as written.
- If `GuideAddition` requires both keys (fields with defaults are generated as required), send `{ glossary: entry, entity: null }` or `{ glossary: null, entity }`. In that case, change the test's expected bodies to include the other key as `null`.

`web/src/review/highlight.ts`:

```ts
export type Piece = { text: string; mark: boolean };

/** Split `text` so every occurrence of every non-empty excerpt is marked (spec §8.4: the judge's
 * error spans highlighted inline). Overlapping marks merge. */
export function highlight(text: string, excerpts: string[]): Piece[] {
  const marked = new Array<boolean>(text.length).fill(false);
  for (const excerpt of excerpts) {
    if (!excerpt) continue;
    for (let at = text.indexOf(excerpt); at !== -1; at = text.indexOf(excerpt, at + 1)) {
      marked.fill(true, at, at + excerpt.length);
    }
  }
  const pieces: Piece[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const last = pieces.at(-1);
    if (last && last.mark === marked[i]) last.text += text[i];
    else pieces.push({ text: text[i]!, mark: marked[i]! });
  }
  return pieces;
}
```

`web/src/review/EditTranslation.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAcceptSpan, useEditSpan } from "../api/review";
import type { SpanDetail } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";

export function EditTranslation({ runId, detail }: { runId: string; detail: SpanDetail }) {
  const edit = useEditSpan(runId);
  const accept = useAcceptSpan(runId);
  const [text, setText] = useState(detail.text);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const changed = text !== detail.text && text.trim() !== "";
  return (
    <div className="edit">
      <label htmlFor={`text-${detail.id}`}>Translation of passage {detail.number}</label>
      <textarea
        id={`text-${detail.id}`}
        dir="auto"
        rows={3}
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
      />
      <div className="row">
        <label htmlFor={`reason-${detail.id}`}>Reason for the edit (optional)</label>
        <input id={`reason-${detail.id}`} value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} />
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={!changed || edit.isPending}
          onClick={() => edit.mutate({ spanId: detail.id, text, reason: reason.trim() || null })}
        >
          Save edit
        </button>
        <button type="button" disabled={accept.isPending} onClick={() => accept.mutate(detail.id)}>
          Accept passage
        </button>
      </div>
      {edit.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={edit.error} /> : null}
      {accept.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={accept.error} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**When the textarea resets.** `SpanPanel` mounts this with `key={detail.text}`. When the saved text (or a chosen candidate) changes the passage, the textarea starts over from the new text. When an action is refused, the text is unchanged and the reviewer's typing stays.

`web/src/review/FlagList.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useFlagStatus } from "../api/review";
import type { SpanDetail } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";

export function FlagList({ runId, detail }: { runId: string; detail: SpanDetail }) {
  const change = useFlagStatus(runId);
  const [dismissing, setDismissing] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  if (detail.flags.length === 0) return <p className="muted">No flags.</p>;
  return (
    <>
      <ul aria-label="Flags" className="flags">
        {detail.flags.map((flag) => (
          <li key={flag.id}>
            <span className={`badge flag-${flag.status}`}>{flag.code}</span> {flag.explanation}{" "}
            <span className="muted">
              ({flag.status}
              {flag.dismiss_reason ? `: ${flag.dismiss_reason}` : ""})
            </span>{" "}
            {flag.status === "open" ? (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={`Accept the ${flag.code} flag`}
                  onClick={() => change.mutate({ flagId: flag.id, status: "accepted", reason: null })}
                >
                  Accept
                </button>{" "}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={`Dismiss the ${flag.code} flag`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setDismissing(flag.id);
                    setReason("");
                  }}
                >
                  Dismiss…
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Reopen the ${flag.code} flag`}
                onClick={() => change.mutate({ flagId: flag.id, status: "open", reason: null })}
              >
                Reopen
              </button>
            )}
            {dismissing === flag.id ? (
              <div className="row">
                <label htmlFor={`dismiss-${flag.id}`}>Why dismiss the {flag.code} flag?</label>
                <input id={`dismiss-${flag.id}`} value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={reason.trim() === ""}
                  onClick={() =>
                    change.mutate(
                      { flagId: flag.id, status: "dismissed", reason: reason.trim() },
                      { onSuccess: () => setDismissing(null) },
                    )
                  }
                >
                  Dismiss
                </button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setDismissing(null)}>
                  Cancel
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {change.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={change.error} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

**How the flags test reads the result.** After the dismissal, the flag's line reads "(dismissed: intended tense)"; the test matches it with a regular expression. After "Reopen", the flag is open again.

`web/src/review/Candidates.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useChooseCandidate } from "../api/review";
import type { SpanDetail } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";

type Choice = { translator: string; model: string; text: string };

/** A translator's candidate can come in pieces (its spans, aligned differently); using it joins
 * them, as the API does. */
function byTranslator(detail: SpanDetail): Choice[] {
  const choices = new Map<string, Choice>();
  for (const c of detail.candidates) {
    const known = choices.get(c.translator);
    if (known) known.text = `${known.text} ${c.text}`;
    else choices.set(c.translator, { translator: c.translator, model: c.model, text: c.text });
  }
  return [...choices.values()];
}

export function Candidates({ runId, detail }: { runId: string; detail: SpanDetail }) {
  const choose = useChooseCandidate(runId);
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Candidates</h3>
      <ul aria-label="Candidates" className="candidates">
        {byTranslator(detail).map((c) => (
          <li key={c.translator}>
            <strong>{c.translator}</strong> <span className="muted">{c.model}</span>
            {c.translator === detail.chosen_from ? <span className="muted"> (chosen)</span> : null}
            <p dir="auto">{c.text}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label={`Use the ${c.translator} translation`}
              disabled={c.text === detail.text || choose.isPending}
              onClick={() => choose.mutate({ spanId: detail.id, translator: c.translator })}
            >
              Use this translation
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {choose.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={choose.error} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

**Joining pieces.** The API joins a translator's pieces with a single space (`review.use_candidate`), so "The cat sleeps." + "It rains." becomes "The cat sleeps. It rains.". That equals the current text, so "Use the literal translation" is disabled. If M4a joins differently, read `use_candidate` in `engine/coglossa/review.py` and join the same way.

`web/src/review/Findings.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SpanDetail } from "../api/types";
import { highlight } from "./highlight";

export function Findings({ detail }: { detail: SpanDetail }) {
  const errors = detail.judge_errors ?? [];
  return (
    <>
      {detail.rationale ? (
        <>
          <h3>Reviewer's rationale</h3>
          <p dir="auto">{detail.rationale}</p>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {detail.issues.length > 0 ? (
        <ul aria-label="Issues">
          {detail.issues.map((issue) => (
            <li key={issue} dir="auto">
              {issue}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
      {errors.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <h3>Judge</h3>
          <p dir="auto" aria-label="Translation with the judge's findings">
            {highlight(detail.text, errors.map((e) => e.excerpt)).map((piece, i) =>
              piece.mark ? <mark key={i}>{piece.text}</mark> : <span key={i}>{piece.text}</span>,
            )}
          </p>
          <ul aria-label="Judge findings">
            {errors.map((e, i) => (
              <li key={i}>
                <span className="badge">{e.severity}</span> {e.category}: {e.explanation}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      ) : detail.judge_errors === null ? (
        <p className="muted">The judge did not assess this passage.</p>
      ) : null}
      {detail.back_translation ? (
        <>
          <h3>Back translation</h3>
          <p dir="auto" className="back-translation">
            {detail.back_translation}
          </p>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/src/review/Signals.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SpanDetail } from "../api/types";
import { formatScore } from "../lib/format";

const value = (v: number | null | undefined) => (v === null || v === undefined ? "—" : v.toFixed(2));

export function Signals({ detail }: { detail: SpanDetail }) {
  const { score } = detail;
  return (
    <table aria-label="Signals" className="signals">
      <tbody>
        <tr>
          <th>meaning</th>
          <td>{formatScore(score.meaning)}</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <th>style</th>
          <td>{formatScore(score.style)}</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <th>reliability</th>
          <td>{score.reliability}</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <th>agreement</th>
          <td>{detail.agreement}</td>
        </tr>
        {Object.entries(score.signals).map(([name, v]) => (
          <tr key={name}>
            <th>{name}</th>
            <td>{value(v)}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

import type { RevisionEntry } from "../api/types";
import { formatDate } from "../lib/format";

export function Revisions({ revisions }: { revisions: RevisionEntry[] }) {
  return (
    <>
      <h3>History</h3>
      <ol aria-label="History" className="history">
        {revisions.map((r, i) => (
          <li key={i}>
            <span className="muted">
              {r.source} · {formatDate(r.created_at)}
              {r.reason ? ` · ${r.reason}` : ""}
            </span>
            <div>
              {r.before !== null ? (
                <>
                  <del dir="auto">{r.before}</del> →{" "}
                </>
              ) : null}
              <ins dir="auto">{r.after}</ins>
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </>
  );
}

`web/src/review/AddToGuide.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useAddToGuide } from "../api/review";
import { emptyEntity } from "../guide/GuideEditor";
import { ENTITY_KINDS } from "../guide/EntityTable";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";

type Props = { runId: string; projectId: string; spanId: string };

export function AddToGuide({ runId, projectId, spanId }: Props) {
  const add = useAddToGuide(runId, projectId);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<"term" | "name">("term");
  const [source, setSource] = useState("");
  const [rendering, setRendering] = useState("");
  const [entityKind, setEntityKind] = useState("person");
  const [locked, setLocked] = useState(false);
  const ready = source.trim() !== "" && rendering.trim() !== "" && !add.isPending;

  function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const addition =
      kind === "term"
        ? { glossary: { term: source.trim(), rendering: rendering.trim(), notes: "", locked } }
        : {
            entity: { ...emptyEntity(), name: source.trim(), kind: entityKind, rendering: rendering.trim(), locked },
          };
    add.mutate(
      { spanId, addition },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setSource("");
          setRendering("");
          setLocked(false);
        },
      },
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="add-to-guide">
      <fieldset>
        <legend>Add to the project guide</legend>
        <label>
          <input type="radio" name={`kind-${spanId}`} checked={kind === "term"} onChange={() => setKind("term")} />{" "}
          Term
        </label>
        <label>
          <input type="radio" name={`kind-${spanId}`} checked={kind === "name"} onChange={() => setKind("name")} />{" "}
          Name
        </label>
        <div className="field">
          <label htmlFor={`source-${spanId}`}>{kind === "term" ? "Source term" : "Name in the source"}</label>
          <input id={`source-${spanId}`} dir="auto" value={source} onChange={(e) => setSource(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="field">
          <label htmlFor={`rendering-${spanId}`}>Rendering</label>
          <input id={`rendering-${spanId}`} dir="auto" value={rendering} onChange={(e) => setRendering(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        {kind === "name" ? (
          <div className="field">
            <label htmlFor={`entity-kind-${spanId}`}>Kind</label>
            <select id={`entity-kind-${spanId}`} value={entityKind} onChange={(e) => setEntityKind(e.target.value)}>
              {ENTITY_KINDS.map((k) => (
                <option key={k} value={k}>
                  {k}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        <label>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={locked} onChange={(e) => setLocked(e.target.checked)} /> Locked
        </label>
        <button type="submit" disabled={!ready}>
          Add to the project guide
        </button>
        {add.isSuccess ? (
          <p role="status">Added to the project guide (version {add.data.info.version}).</p>
        ) : null}
        {add.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={add.error} /> : null}
      </fieldset>
    </form>
  );
}
```

**The guide form's accessible names.** The `<fieldset>` with a `<legend>` gets the role `group`, named by its legend, which is what the test looks up. The radio labelled "Name" and the field labelled "Name in the source" are different labels, and exact matching keeps them apart.

`web/src/review/SpanPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useSpanDetail } from "../api/review";
import type { ReviewSpan } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../components/Loading";
import { AddToGuide } from "./AddToGuide";
import { Candidates } from "./Candidates";
import { EditTranslation } from "./EditTranslation";
import { Findings } from "./Findings";
import { FlagList } from "./FlagList";
import { Revisions } from "./Revisions";
import { Signals } from "./Signals";

type Props = { runId: string; projectId: string; span: ReviewSpan };

export function SpanPanel({ runId, projectId, span }: Props) {
  const detail = useSpanDetail(runId, span.id);
  if (detail.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (detail.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={detail.error} />;
  const d = detail.data;
  return (
    <section aria-label={`Passage ${d.number}`} className="span-panel">
      <EditTranslation key={d.text} runId={runId} detail={d} />
      <h3>Flags</h3>
      <FlagList runId={runId} detail={d} />
      <div className="two-columns">
        <div>
          <Candidates runId={runId} detail={d} />
          <Findings detail={d} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <Signals detail={d} />
          <Revisions revisions={d.revisions} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <AddToGuide runId={runId} projectId={projectId} spanId={d.id} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

### Task 11: Export, and the System page

This task covers two screens:
- **Export** (spec §8.5 and §7.5): the run's export tab links to the four exports. Each link is a plain download from the API, which builds the file from the current revisions. The bilingual Word export can leave out the flag comments.
- **System** (spec §8.6 and §7.4): `doctor`'s checks, run on request (a probe of each model's context window is optional and slow); the active config, read-only; and the language tier table.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/api/system.ts`, `web/src/routes.tsx`, `web/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `web/src/styles/app.css`
- Create: `web/src/pages/run/ExportPanel.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SystemPage.tsx`
- Test: `web/src/pages/run/ExportPanel.test.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SystemPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 3–6): `exportUrl`, `useRunDetail`, `client`, `unwrap`, the fixtures.
- Produces:
  - `useDoctor()`: a mutation `(probe: boolean) => DoctorReport`.
  - `useConfig()` and `useTiers()`: queries with keys `["system", "config"]` and `["system", "tiers"]`.
  - Routes: `/runs/:runId/export` → `ExportPanel`, and `/system` → `SystemPage`.
  - Fixtures: `doctorReport: DoctorReport`, `configView: ConfigView`, `tiers: TierTable`.

`web/src/pages/SystemPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { http, HttpResponse } from "msw";
import { configView, doctorReport, tiers } from "../test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "../test/render";
import { server } from "../test/server";

function serve() {
  const probes: (string | null)[] = [];
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/system/config", () => HttpResponse.json(configView)),
    http.get("*/api/system/tiers", () => HttpResponse.json(tiers)),
    http.get("*/api/system/doctor", ({ request }) => {
      probes.push(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("probe"));
      return HttpResponse.json(doctorReport);
    }),
  );
  return probes;
}

it("shows the active config and the tier table", async () => {
  serve();
  renderApp("/system");
  expect(await screen.findByText("/etc/coglossa/coglossa.yaml")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText(/3f2a9c/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText(/"max_upload_mb": 200/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  const languages = screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Languages" });
  expect(within(languages).getByRole("row", { name: "am C" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  const pairs = screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Language pairs" });
  expect(within(pairs).getByRole("row", { name: "fr>en A" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText("Pairs not listed here are tier B.")).toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("runs the checks on request, with the slow probe as an option", async () => {
  const probes = serve();
  const { user } = renderApp("/system");
  expect(probes).toEqual([]); // doctor calls the backends: never on page load
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Run the checks" }));
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Checks" });
  expect(screen.getByText("Some checks need attention.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  const scorer = within(table).getByRole("row", { name: /scorer/ });
  expect(within(scorer).getByText("fail")).toHaveClass("check-fail");
  expect(scorer).toHaveTextContent("start the scorer service");
  await user.click(screen.getByLabelText(/Also test each model's context window/));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Run the checks" }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(probes).toEqual(["false", "true"]));
});

it("says when everything passes", async () => {
  serve();
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/system/doctor", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ ok: true, checks: [doctorReport.checks[0]] }),
    ),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/system");
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Run the checks" }));
  expect(await screen.findByText("All checks passed.")).toBeInTheDocument();
});

it("shows what could not load", async () => {
  server.use(
    http.get("*/api/system/config", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ detail: "config unreadable" }, { status: 500 }),
    ),
    http.get("*/api/system/tiers", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ detail: "tiers unreadable" }, { status: 500 }),
    ),
    http.get("*/api/system/doctor", () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ detail: "the database is unavailable: x" }, { status: 503 }),
    ),
  );
  const { user } = renderApp("/system");
  expect(await screen.findByText("config unreadable")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(await screen.findByText("tiers unreadable")).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Run the checks" }));
  expect(await screen.findByText("the database is unavailable: x")).toBeInTheDocument();
});
```

The 500 answers are retried once in production. Here `renderApp` uses `retry: false`, so they fail at once.

**Doctor's probe parameter.** The test expects `probe=false` on the first run: the client always sends the flag. If openapi-fetch drops `false` booleans from the query, change the first expectation to `null`; the checkbox behaviour is what matters.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

Append to `web/src/api/system.ts` (import `useMutation`):

```ts
export function useDoctor() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (probe: boolean) =>
      unwrap(client.GET("/api/system/doctor", { params: { query: { probe } } })),
  });
}

export function useConfig() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["system", "config"],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(client.GET("/api/system/config")),
  });
}

export function useTiers() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["system", "tiers"],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(client.GET("/api/system/tiers")),
  });
}
```

`web/src/pages/run/ExportPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { exportUrl } from "../../api/client";
import { useRunDetail } from "./RunLayout";

export function ExportPanel() {
  const detail = useRunDetail();
  const [comments, setComments] = useState(true);
  if (!detail.report_available) {
    return <p className="muted">Exports are available once the translation is complete.</p>;
  }
  const runId = detail.run.id;
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="muted">
        Exports are built when you download them, so they include every edit made in review.
      </p>
      <ul className="exports">
        <li>
          <a href={exportUrl(runId, "html")} download>
            Web page (HTML)
          </a>{" "}
          <span className="muted">one file with the summary, scores and flags</span>
        </li>
        <li>
          <a href={exportUrl(runId, "bilingual-docx", comments)} download>
            Word, source and translation side by side
          </a>
          <label>
            <input type="checkbox" checked={comments} onChange={(e) => setComments(e.target.checked)} />{" "}
            Add open flags as Word comments
          </label>
        </li>
        <li>
          <a href={exportUrl(runId, "docx")} download>
            Word, translation only
          </a>
        </li>
        <li>
          <a href={exportUrl(runId, "json")} download>
            Report (JSON)
          </a>{" "}
          <span className="muted">everything the run produced, including the model calls</span>
        </li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

**The `download` attribute.** The server sends `Content-Disposition` with the file's name (RFC 5987, M4a), and that name wins over the attribute. The attribute only makes the browser download instead of navigating.

`web/src/pages/SystemPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useConfig, useDoctor, useTiers } from "../api/system";
import type { TierTable } from "../api/types";
import { ErrorMessage } from "../components/ErrorMessage";
import { Loading } from "../components/Loading";

function Doctor() {
  const doctor = useDoctor();
  const [probe, setProbe] = useState(false);
  return (
    <section aria-label="Installation checks">
      <h2>Installation checks</h2>
      <p className="muted">
        Checks the config, every model backend, the scorer, the database and the storage.
      </p>
      <label>
        <input type="checkbox" checked={probe} onChange={(e) => setProbe(e.target.checked)} /> Also
        test each model's context window (slow)
      </label>
      <button type="button" disabled={doctor.isPending} onClick={() => doctor.mutate(probe)}>
        Run the checks
      </button>
      {doctor.isPending ? <Loading /> : null}
      {doctor.isError ? <ErrorMessage error={doctor.error} /> : null}
      {doctor.data ? (
        <>
          <p className={doctor.data.ok ? "check-pass" : "check-fail"}>
            {doctor.data.ok ? "All checks passed." : "Some checks need attention."}
          </p>
          <table aria-label="Checks">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Check</th>
                <th>Result</th>
                <th>Details</th>
                <th>What to do</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {doctor.data.checks.map((check) => (
                <tr key={check.name}>
                  <td>{check.name}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`badge check-${check.status}`}>{check.status}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>{check.message}</td>
                  <td>{check.fix}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function Config() {
  const config = useConfig();
  if (config.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (config.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={config.error} />;
  return (
    <>
      <p>
        <code>{config.data.path}</code> <span className="muted">(hash {config.data.hash})</span>
      </p>
      <pre className="config">{JSON.stringify(config.data.config, null, 2)}</pre>
    </>
  );
}

function TierRows({ entries }: { entries: [string, string][] }) {
  return (
    <tbody>
      {entries.map(([key, tier]) => (
        <tr key={key}>
          <td>{key}</td>
          <td>{tier}</td>
        </tr>
      ))}
    </tbody>
  );
}

function Tiers() {
  const tiers = useTiers();
  if (tiers.isPending) return <Loading />;
  if (tiers.isError) return <ErrorMessage error={tiers.error} />;
  const t: TierTable = tiers.data;
  return (
    <>
      <p>Pairs not listed here are tier {t.default}.</p>
      <div className="two-columns">
        <table aria-label="Languages">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Language</th>
              <th>Tier</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <TierRows entries={Object.entries(t.languages)} />
        </table>
        <table aria-label="Language pairs">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Pair</th>
              <th>Tier</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <TierRows entries={Object.entries(t.pairs)} />
        </table>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function SystemPage() {
  return (
    <section>
      <h1>System</h1>
      <Doctor />
      <h2>Active configuration</h2>
      <p className="muted">Read-only: change coglossa.yaml on the server and restart the services.</p>
      <Config />
      <h2>Language reliability tiers</h2>
      <Tiers />
    </section>
  );
}
```

**Tier keys.** If the API's tier table types `languages` and `pairs` as `Record<string, Reliability>`, `Object.entries` gives `[string, Reliability][]`, which `TierRows` accepts as `[string, string][]`.

### Task 12: A local stack, the Playwright journeys, the e2e CI job, and the docs

Spec §9.1 calls for Playwright journeys: upload → approve guide → review and resolve a flag → export.

**The local stack.** The journeys need the real system. `api/devstack.py` starts one:
- a throwaway Postgres database, migrated
- the fake OpenAI-compatible server and the fake scorer (`coglossa fake-openai`, `coglossa fake-scorer`)
- a worker (`coglossa worker`)
- the API serving the built web app (`coglossa-api` with `api.web_dir`).

Ctrl+C or SIGTERM stops it all and drops the database. Playwright starts it as its web server. A developer runs it with `make dev-stack`, alongside `pnpm dev` for live reloading.

**What the journeys check:**
- **The flag.** The source text contains the synthetic marker `MISTRANSLATED` (Task 1), so a flag appears to be resolved.
- **Offline (Review Focus).** Every request the browser makes goes to the Coglossa server itself: the fonts and assets are bundled, and nothing is fetched from elsewhere.

**Docs.** This task also writes the README's web section and `docs/m4b-followups.md`, and moves the M4a follow-ups that M4b resolved.

**Files:**
- Create: `api/devstack.py`, `web/playwright.config.ts`, `web/e2e/journey.spec.ts`, `web/e2e/system.spec.ts`, `docs/m4b-followups.md`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (an `e2e` job), `README.md`, `docs/m4a-followups.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - **Engine:** `coglossa.testing.postgres.create_test_database` / `drop_test_database` / `TEST_DB_ENV` and `coglossa.store.migrate.upgrade(url)`.
  - **CLI commands:** `coglossa fake-openai --host --port`, `coglossa fake-scorer --host --port`, `coglossa worker --config`, `coglossa-api --config --host --port`.
  - **Task 2:** `api.web_dir`.
  - **The web app:** its labels and routes from Tasks 3–11.
- Produces:
  - `cd api && uv run python devstack.py [--host 127.0.0.1] [--port 8080] [--web-dir PATH]`. It prints `Coglossa is running at http://<host>:<port>`, and `GET /api/health` answers 200 once it is ready.
  - `pnpm e2e` (via `make e2e`).
  - The CI job `e2e`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The local stack**

Check the CLI first:
- `cd api && uv run coglossa fake-openai --help` and `uv run coglossa fake-scorer --help` should list `--host` and `--port`.
- `uv run coglossa worker --help` should list `--config`.

If a flag is named differently, use the real name below.

`api/devstack.py`:

```python
"""A complete local Coglossa for a browser: a throwaway database, the fake model and scorer
servers, a worker, and the API serving the built web app. `make dev-stack` runs it for
development (with `pnpm dev` for live reloading); the Playwright journeys start it as their web
server. Ctrl+C (or SIGTERM) stops everything and drops the database."""

import argparse
import contextlib
import os
import shutil
import signal
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from types import FrameType

import yaml
from coglossa.store.migrate import upgrade
from coglossa.testing.postgres import TEST_DB_ENV, create_test_database, drop_test_database

COGLOSSA = [sys.executable, "-c", "import sys; from coglossa.cli import main; sys.exit(main())"]
API = [sys.executable, "-c", "import sys; from coglossa_api.main import main; sys.exit(main())"]


def free_port() -> int:
    with socket.socket() as s:
        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        return s.getsockname()[1]


def wait_for(url: str, timeout_s: float, procs: list[subprocess.Popen[bytes]]) -> None:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        for p in procs:
            if p.poll() is not None:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{p.args} exited with {p.returncode}")
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=1) as response:  # noqa: S310 (local URL)
                if response.status == 200:
                    return
        time.sleep(0.2)
    raise RuntimeError(f"{url} did not answer within {timeout_s:.0f} s")


def config(work: Path, llm_port: int, scorer_port: int, web_dir: Path | None) -> dict:
    roles = {
        role: {"model": "m1"}
        for role in ("literal_translator", "contextual_translator", "reviewer", "profiler_guide")
    }
    roles |= {"judge": {"model": "m2"}, "roundtrip": {"model": "m2"}}
    api: dict = {"progress_poll_s": 0.2}
    if web_dir is not None:
        api["web_dir"] = str(web_dir.resolve())
    return {
        "backends": {"local": {"base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{llm_port}/v1", "max_concurrency": 4}},
        "models": {
            "m1": {"backend": "local", "name": "fake-model", "context_window": 32768},
            "m2": {"backend": "local", "name": "fake-model", "context_window": 32768},
        },
        "roles": roles,
        "scorer": {"url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{scorer_port}"},
        "storage": {"path": str(work / "files")},
        "api": api,
        "profiles": {"sweep": {"short": True}},  # demo documents are short; keep the sweep on
    }


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8080)
    parser.add_argument("--web-dir", type=Path, help="the built web app (web/dist)")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    admin = os.environ.get(TEST_DB_ENV)
    if not admin:
        print(f"error: set {TEST_DB_ENV} to a Postgres admin URL (make dev-stack does)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    url = create_test_database(admin)
    work = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="coglossa-dev-"))
    procs: list[subprocess.Popen[bytes]] = []

    def stop(signum: int | None = None, frame: FrameType | None = None) -> None:
        raise KeyboardInterrupt

    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, stop)
    try:
        upgrade(url)
        llm, scorer = free_port(), free_port()
        path = work / "coglossa.yaml"
        path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(config(work, llm, scorer, args.web_dir)), encoding="utf-8")
        env = {**os.environ, "COGLOSSA_DATABASE_URL": url}
        host = ["--host", "127.0.0.1"]
        procs.append(subprocess.Popen([*COGLOSSA, "fake-openai", *host, "--port", str(llm)], env=env))
        procs.append(subprocess.Popen([*COGLOSSA, "fake-scorer", *host, "--port", str(scorer)], env=env))
        procs.append(subprocess.Popen([*COGLOSSA, "worker", "--config", str(path)], env=env))
        procs.append(
            subprocess.Popen(
                [*API, "--config", str(path), "--host", args.host, "--port", str(args.port)], env=env
            )
        )
        wait_for(f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}/api/health", 60, procs)
        print(f"Coglossa is running at http://{args.host}:{args.port} (Ctrl+C to stop)", flush=True)
        while all(p.poll() is None for p in procs):
            time.sleep(0.5)
        print("error: a service stopped; shutting down", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        return 0
    finally:
        for p in procs:
            p.terminate()
        for p in procs:
            with contextlib.suppress(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
                p.wait(timeout=10)
            if p.poll() is None:
                p.kill()
        drop_test_database(admin, url)
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

**Notes on the script:**
- **Ruff.** `ruff check` covers this file; adjust the `noqa` and any line-length issues with `make fmt`.
- **Coverage.** The file is outside `coglossa_api`, so it is not in the API's coverage gate. The journeys exercise it.
- **Sweep config key.** If `profiles: {sweep: {short: True}}` is not the config's shape, copy what M4a's `api/tests/test_integration.py` uses; it is the same.
- **Worker startup.** If `coglossa worker` needs the database migrated before it starts, `upgrade(url)` has already run.

Try it:

```bash
make db-up
cd web && pnpm build && cd ../api
COGLOSSA_TEST_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://coglossa:coglossa@127.0.0.1:55432/postgres uv run python devstack.py --port 8765 --web-dir ../web/dist
```

Expected: it prints "Coglossa is running at http://127.0.0.1:8765". Opening that address in a browser shows the projects page; a short `.txt` upload completes within seconds. Ctrl+C returns to the prompt, and `psql -l` no longer lists the `coglossa_test_*` database.
