```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from "@playwright/test";

const PORT = 8765;

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "./e2e",
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1, // one stack, one database
  timeout: 90_000,
  expect: { timeout: 30_000 }, // runs take a few seconds against the fake models
  reporter: process.env["CI"] ? [["list"], ["html", { open: "never" }]] : "list",
  use: { baseURL: `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`, trace: "retain-on-failure" },
  projects: [{ name: "chromium", use: { ...devices["Desktop Chrome"] } }],
  webServer: {
    command: `cd ../api && uv run python devstack.py --host 127.0.0.1 --port ${PORT} --web-dir ../web/dist`,
    url: `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/health`,
    timeout: 120_000,
    reuseExistingServer: false,
    stdout: "pipe",
  },
});
```

`web/e2e/journey.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page, type Request } from "@playwright/test";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";

const STORY = [
  "Le chat dort près de la fenêtre. Il pleut sur la ville.",
  "Marie ouvre la porte. Le chat MISTRANSLATED se réveille.",
].join("\n\n");

function watchRequests(page: Page, origin: string): Request[] {
  const outside: Request[] = [];
  page.on("request", (request) => {
    if (!request.url().startsWith(origin) && !request.url().startsWith("data:")) outside.push(request);
  });
  return outside;
}

test("upload, approve the guide, resolve a flag and export", async ({ page, baseURL }) => {
  const outside = watchRequests(page, baseURL!);
  const name = `Journey ${Date.now()}`;

  // Create a project.
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/projects$/);
  await page.getByLabel("Name", { exact: true }).fill(name);
  await page.getByLabel("Target language").fill("en");
  await page.getByLabel("Source language").fill("fr");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create project" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name })).toBeVisible();

  // Upload a document and stop at the guide.
  await page.getByLabel("Document").setInputFiles({
    name: "histoire.txt",
    mimeType: "text/plain",
    buffer: Buffer.from(STORY, "utf-8"),
  });
  await page.getByLabel(/Stop for guide review/).check();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Upload and translate" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/runs\/[0-9a-f-]+$/);
  await expect(page.getByText("Waiting for guide approval", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  // Edit and approve the guide.
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Review and approve the guide" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add term" }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Term 1", { exact: true }).fill("fenêtre");
  await page.getByLabel("Rendering 1", { exact: true }).fill("window");
  await page.getByLabel("Lock term 1", { exact: true }).check();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve and translate" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText("Complete", { exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });

  // Review: find the flagged passage, fix it and dismiss its flag with a reason.
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Review", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Only passages with open flags").check();
  await page.getByLabel("Flag", { exact: true }).selectOption("low_meaning");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Apply filters" }).click();
  const row = page.getByRole("row").filter({ hasText: "MISTRANSLATED" }).first();
  await row.getByRole("button", { name: /Details of passage/ }).click();
  const panel = page.getByRole("region", { name: /Passage \d+/ });
  const box = panel.getByLabel(/Translation of passage/);
  await box.fill("Marie opens the door. The cat wakes up.");
  await panel.getByLabel("Reason for the edit (optional)").fill("the marker was a test error");
  await panel.getByRole("button", { name: "Save edit" }).click();
  await expect(panel.getByRole("list", { name: "History" })).toContainText("the marker was a test error");
  await panel.getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss the low_meaning flag" }).click();
  await panel.getByLabel("Why dismiss the low_meaning flag?").fill("fixed by the edit");
  await panel.getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(panel.getByText(/dismissed: fixed by the edit/)).toBeVisible();

  // Export: the edit is in the downloads.
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Export", exact: true }).click();
  const [html] = await Promise.all([
    page.waitForEvent("download"),
    page.getByRole("link", { name: "Web page (HTML)" }).click(),
  ]);
  expect(html.suggestedFilename()).toMatch(/\.html$/);
  const text = await readFile((await html.path())!, "utf-8");
  expect(text).toContain("Marie opens the door. The cat wakes up.");
  const [docx] = await Promise.all([
    page.waitForEvent("download"),
    page.getByRole("link", { name: "Word, translation only" }).click(),
  ]);
  expect(docx.suggestedFilename()).toMatch(/\.docx$/);

  // Spec §8: the app works offline: nothing was requested from anywhere else.
  expect(outside.map((r) => r.url())).toEqual([]);
});

test("a reload keeps the review's place", async ({ page }) => {
  // Reuses whichever finished run the projects list shows first; the journey above made one.
  await page.goto("/projects");
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: /^Journey / }).first().click();
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Open run" }).first().click();
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Review", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Only passages with open flags").check();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Apply filters" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/flagged=1/);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Only passages with open flags")).toBeChecked();
});
```

**Notes on the journey:**
- **Where the flag lands.** The marker sits in the second paragraph. The synthetic translation of every segment is `[en] <source>`, so its translation contains `MISTRANSLATED`. That passage gets a critical judge error, which aggregation turns into an open `low_meaning` flag.
- **Exact labels.** Playwright's `getByLabel` matches substrings case-insensitively by default, so "Flag" would also match "Only passages with open flags", and "Term 1" would match "Lock term 1". The short labels therefore use `{ exact: true }`.
- **The other passages.** The locked term "fenêtre" → "window" is not in "[en] Le chat dort près de la fenêtre…". The first passage therefore gets a `terminology_violation`, and the sweep then appends the rendering (the synthetic sweep's behaviour). The filter on `low_meaning` keeps the journey on the marked passage.
- **The second test** depends on the first having run. Playwright runs them in file order with `workers: 1`. If ordering ever changes, fold the second test's steps into the end of the first.

`web/e2e/system.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

test("the System page runs the checks against the live stack", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/system");
  await expect(page.getByText(/coglossa\.yaml/)).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Run the checks" }).click();
  const checks = page.getByRole("table", { name: "Checks" });
  await expect(checks).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
  await expect(checks.getByRole("row", { name: /database/ })).toContainText("pass");
  await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Language pairs" })).toBeVisible();
});

test("an unknown address shows the app's not-found page", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/no/such/page");
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Page not found" })).toBeVisible();
});
```

**The doctor's check names.** Read `engine/coglossa/doctor.py` for the name of the database check. If it is not "database", match the real name in the row filter.

Run:

```bash
cd web && pnpm exec playwright install chromium
make e2e
```

Expected: 4 passed.

**If `playwright install` hangs.** On Node 26 it can hang while extracting the archive. The Chromium build Playwright 1.56.1 needs (`chromium_headless_shell-1194`) may already be in `~/Library/Caches/ms-playwright` from the planning spike. If it is, skip the install. CI uses Node 22.

- [ ] **Step 3: The e2e CI job**

Append to `.github/workflows/ci.yml`:

```yaml
  e2e:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:16-alpine
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: coglossa
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: coglossa
          POSTGRES_DB: coglossa
        ports: ["5432:5432"]
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U coglossa"
          --health-interval 2s --health-timeout 3s --health-retries 30
    env:
      COGLOSSA_TEST_DATABASE_URL: postgresql://coglossa:coglossa@127.0.0.1:5432/postgres
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v6
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"
      - run: uv sync --all-groups
        working-directory: api
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          package_json_file: web/package.json
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: "22"
          cache: pnpm
          cache-dependency-path: web/pnpm-lock.yaml
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
        working-directory: web
      - run: pnpm exec playwright install --with-deps chromium
        working-directory: web
      - run: pnpm build
        working-directory: web
      - run: pnpm e2e
        working-directory: web
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: failure()
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: web/playwright-report
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Docs**

`README.md`:
1. **Status line.** Change it to `Status: **M4** (engine + CLI + scoring + queued runs + REST API + web app)`.
2. **Web app section.** Add a "Web app" section after the REST API section:

```markdown
## Web app

The web app (`web/`, React + Vite) is how reviewers use Coglossa:
- **Projects:** create projects and upload documents.
- **Runs:** follow runs live.
- **Guide:** edit and approve the guide.
- **Review:** review every passage with its scores, flags, candidates and history.
- **Exports:** download the exports.
- **System:** run the installation checks.

The API serves it when `api.web_dir` points at the built files.

    cd web && pnpm install && pnpm build      # web/dist
    # coglossa.yaml:  api: { web_dir: ./web/dist }

It works offline: the fonts (Noto) are bundled and nothing is loaded from the internet. Every passage is shown in its own direction (`dir="auto"`), so right-to-left text displays correctly.

**Development:**
- `make dev-stack` runs a complete local Coglossa on port 8080: a throwaway database, fake models, a worker and the API.
- `cd web && pnpm dev` serves the app with live reloading and forwards `/api` to that stack.
- `make test-web` runs the component tests (Vitest, 95% coverage gate).
- `make e2e` runs the Playwright journeys against a fresh stack.
- `make schema` regenerates `web/src/api/schema.d.ts` from `schema/openapi.json`, and CI fails when they differ.
```

- **The SSE client must close the stream on a terminal state.** The web client closes it (Task 6).

Create `docs/m4b-followups.md`:

```markdown
# M4b follow-ups

## Should fix soon (M5)
- **The API image must build the web app.** It must set `api.web_dir` and run `pnpm build` in a build stage; the M5 Dockerfile owns this.
- **Font coverage.** The bundle carries Noto Sans for Latin, Cyrillic, Greek, Devanagari, Vietnamese, Arabic, Hebrew, Bengali, Thai, Ethiopic, and Simplified Chinese, Japanese and Korean (regular weight for the last six). A target language in another script falls back to the system's fonts. Add the customer's scripts (`@fontsource/noto-sans-<script>`) when they are known.

## Later
- **UI language:** the UI is English only (spec §8). The strings are inline; extracting them is a prerequisite for translating the UI.
- **Keyboard shortcuts** cover moving between flags and opening passages. Shortcuts for the actions themselves (accept, dismiss) are not there yet.
- **Unsaved-changes guard:** only the guide editor has one. The passage editor keeps its text across a refused save, but leaving the page drops an unsaved edit.
- **Many runs in a project:** the projects list and a project's documents load in full. Paging can wait until a project has hundreds of documents.
- **Run comparison:** side-by-side comparison of runs is a `bench` concern in v1 (spec §8.2).

## Spec deviations in M4b
- None known.
```

## Self-review notes (plan author)

**Spec coverage (§8):**

| Screen | Spec items | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Projects | create; source auto-detect or override; profile auto or override | Task 4 |
| Documents and runs | upload | Task 5 |
| | status, live progress by stage, retry, cancel | Task 6 |
| | the runs of each document | Task 5 |
| Guide editor | summary, glossary (edit, lock), names, style guide, cultural references | Task 7 |
| | Approve resumes the run | Task 7 |
| | a locked change offers to re-run the sweep | Task 8 |
| Review | the five columns, the filters, keyboard navigation, the document header (summary, reliability banner, statistics) | Task 9 |
| | the panel: candidates with role and model, rationale, signals, judge highlights, history | Task 10 |
| | the actions: accept, use candidate, edit, dismiss with a reason (and accept or reopen), add to guide | Task 10 |
| Export | the four exports | Task 11 |
| System | doctor, config, tiers | Task 11 |

**Cross-cutting items:**
- **Offline fonts:** Task 3; asserted in Task 12.
- **`dir="auto"`:** throughout; Review Focus 2.
- **The API serves the SPA:** Task 2.
- **TypeScript types from the schema** with a CI drift check: Task 3.
- **Vitest + Testing Library + Playwright** and the 95% gate: Tasks 3 and 12.
- **The journey** "upload → approve guide → review and resolve a flag → export": Task 12.

**M4a carry-over:**

| Item | Where |
|---|---|
| Checkpoint approval with more than one run in a project | Task 1 (one definition of the project guide) |
| SSE client closes on a terminal state | Task 6 |
| README `base_version` | Task 2 |
| Auth route test walks the real routes | Task 2 |
| Guide bodies cleaned | Task 1 |

**Choices made here that a reviewer may question:**
- **One definition of the project guide.** It is `runs.prior_guide`, the guide new runs build on: approved, else the latest finished run's. Engine drafts of waiting, failed or cancelled runs no longer count, which fixes the M4a gap.
  - **Checkpoint approval is refused only when the project guide is newer than the base.** A draft is normally newer than the project guide it was built from, so an equality check would refuse every approval.
  - **Cost:** a person who wants to approve over a project guide saved after the draft must start from that guide. The UI offers that (Task 7).
- **Older major versions on purpose.** react-router 8, Vite 8, Vitest 5, msw 3, ESLint 10 and TypeScript 7 exist. The plan pins the last majors whose APIs were verified in a spike: react-router 7, Vite 7, Vitest 3.2, msw 2, ESLint 9, TypeScript 5.9. All are maintained and at least two weeks old. Upgrading is a separate change.
- **No UI component library.** Plain CSS and native elements keep the offline bundle small and the accessible names under our control.
- **Polling fallback is 3 s, client side.** Server-sent events are the main path; the fallback exists for environments without `EventSource`.
- **CJK, Thai, Ethiopic and Bengali fonts ship in the regular weight only.** Bold is synthesised. This keeps `dist/` smaller; recorded in `docs/m4b-followups.md`.

**Spike evidence.** Every unusual line in Task 3 comes from a spike that ran these exact versions:
- the late-bound fetch
- `location.origin`
- the jsdom environment that keeps Node's `AbortController`
- the Node `FormData`/`File` stubs in the upload tests
- `onlyBuiltDependencies`
- the Playwright host.

The spike also confirmed that the coverage gate fails on an untested file and that the built app contains no external URLs.
